namespace DevExpress.Xpf.Core.Native
{
	public class PreloadProvider : IPreloadSupport
	{
		private class Item
		{
			public string Name { get; set; }

			public string Surname { get; set; }

			public int Value { get; set; }
		}

		IEnumerable<FrameworkElement> IPreloadSupport.GetConfiguredTree()
		{
			yield return new TreeMapControl
			{
				Titles = 
				{
					new Title
					{
						Content = "Tittle"
					}
				},
				Legend = new DevExpress.Xpf.TreeMap.Legend
				{
					Title = new LegendTitle
					{
						Content = "LegendTitle"
					}
				},
				DataAdapter = new TreeMapFlatDataAdapter
				{
					DataSource = new Item[3]
					{
						new Item
						{
							Name = "A",
							Surname = "aaa",
							Value = 100
						},
						new Item
						{
							Name = "B",
							Surname = "aaa",
							Value = 300
						},
						new Item
						{
							Name = "C",
							Surname = "bbb",
							Value = 400
						}
					},
					Groups = 
					{
						new TreeMapGroupDefinition
						{
							GroupDataMember = "Surname"
						}
					}
				},
				Colorizer = new TreeMapPaletteColorizer
				{
					ColorizeGroups = true,
					Palette = new Office2016Palette()
				}
			};
		}
	}
}
namespace DevExpress.Xpf.TreeMap
{
	public enum PaletteScaleMode
	{
		Repeat,
		Extrapolate
	}
	[TypeConverter(typeof(PaletteTypeConverter))]
	public abstract class PaletteBase : TreeMapDependencyObject
	{
		private class ColorIndexCache : ColorIndexCacheCore
		{
			private readonly Dictionary<int, Color> convertedColorCache = new Dictionary<int, Color>();

			public Color ConvertColor(Color color, int index, int actualIndex)
			{
				if (convertedColorCache.TryGetValue(index, out var value))
				{
					return value;
				}
				int actualIndexRepeatCount = GetActualIndexRepeatCount(index, actualIndex);
				value = ((actualIndexRepeatCount == 0) ? color : ConvertColor(color.ToColorARGB(), actualIndexRepeatCount).ToColor());
				convertedColorCache[index] = value;
				return value;
			}
		}

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScaleModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ScaleMode", typeof(PaletteScaleMode), typeof(PaletteBase), new PropertyMetadata(PaletteScaleMode.Repeat));

		private readonly ColorIndexCache indexCache = new ColorIndexCache();

		public PaletteScaleMode ScaleMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (PaletteScaleMode)GetValue(ScaleModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ScaleModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected internal abstract ColorCollection ActualColors { get; }

		public Color this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				if (Count < 1)
				{
					return Colors.Transparent;
				}
				int num = index % ActualColors.Count;
				if (ScaleMode == PaletteScaleMode.Repeat)
				{
					return ActualColors[num];
				}
				return indexCache.ConvertColor(ActualColors[num], index, num);
			}
		}

		public int Count
		{
			get
			{
				if (ActualColors == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return ActualColors.Count;
			}
		}

		public virtual string PaletteName => null;
	}
	[ContentProperty("Colors")]
	public class CustomPalette : PaletteBase
	{
		private readonly ColorCollection colors;

		protected internal override ColorCollection ActualColors => Colors;

		public ColorCollection Colors => colors;

		public override string PaletteName => "Custom";

		public CustomPalette()
		{
			colors = new ColorCollection();
			colors.CollectionChanged += delegate
			{
				NotifyPropertyChanged("Colors");
			};
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new CustomPalette();
		}
	}
	[TypeConverter(typeof(ColorCollectionTypeConverter))]
	public class ColorCollection : ObservableCollection<Color>, IFormattable
	{
		public string ToString(string format, IFormatProvider formatProvider)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			using (IEnumerator<Color> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					list.Add(enumerator.Current.ToString(formatProvider));
				}
			}
			return string.Join(" ", list);
		}
	}
	public class PredefinedPalette : PaletteBase
	{
		private readonly ColorCollection predefinedColors = new ColorCollection();

		protected internal override ColorCollection ActualColors => predefinedColors;

		private PredefinedPalette()
		{
		}

		protected PredefinedPalette(Color[] colors)
		{
			foreach (Color item in colors)
			{
				predefinedColors.Add(item);
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new PredefinedPalette();
		}
	}

	public class TreeMapGradientColorizer : TreeMapColorizerBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty StartColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("StartColor", typeof(Color), typeof(TreeMapGradientColorizer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Colors.Black));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty EndColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("EndColor", typeof(Color), typeof(TreeMapGradientColorizer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Colors.White));

		public Color StartColor
		{
			get
			{
				return (Color)GetValue(StartColorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(StartColorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Color EndColor
		{
			get
			{
				return (Color)GetValue(EndColorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(EndColorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapGradientColorizer();
		}

		public override Color? GetItemColor(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			if (!item.IsGroup)
			{
				double num = group.MaxValue - group.MinValue;
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					if (item.Value < group.MinValue)
					{
						return StartColor;
					}
					if (item.Value > group.MaxValue)
					{
						return EndColor;
					}
					double ratio = (group.MaxValue - item.Value) / num;
					return ColorizerCalculator.MixColors(StartColor, EndColor, ratio);
				}
				return StartColor;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer : TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Min", typeof(double), typeof(TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.5, null, CoerceMin));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaxProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Max", typeof(double), typeof(TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.9, null, CoerceMin));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GradientColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GradientColor", typeof(Color), typeof(TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer), new PropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent));

		public double Min
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MinProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MinProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double Max
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MaxProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaxProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Color GradientColor
		{
			get
			{
				return (Color)GetValue(GradientColorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GradientColorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceMin(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 1.0;
			double num3 = (double)value;
			if (num3 > num2)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			if (num3 < num)
			{
				return num;
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer();
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return base.Behavior.CreateLegendItems(sourceItems, varyColorInGroup: false, (ILegendSourceItem item) => WpfLegendItemCreator.CreateLegendItemGradientBrush(item, GradientColor));
		}

		public override Color? GetItemColor(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			if (!item.IsGroup && base.Palette != null && base.Palette.Count > 0)
			{
				double ratio = ((group.MaxValue > group.MinValue) ? (Max - (group.MaxValue - item.Value) * ((Max - Min) / (group.MaxValue - group.MinValue))) : Max);
				return ColorizerCalculator.MixColors(GradientColor, base.Palette[group.GroupIndex], ratio);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapPaletteColorizer : TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColorizeGroupsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ColorizeGroups", typeof(bool), typeof(TreeMapPaletteColorizer), new PropertyMetadata(false));

		public bool ColorizeGroups
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ColorizeGroupsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ColorizeGroupsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapPaletteColorizer();
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return base.Behavior.CreateLegendItems(sourceItems, !ColorizeGroups, WpfLegendItemCreator.CreateLegendItemBrush);
		}

		public override Color? GetItemColor(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			if (!item.IsGroup && base.Palette != null && base.Palette.Count > 0)
			{
				return ColorizeGroups ? base.Palette[group.GroupIndex] : base.Palette[group.ItemIndex];
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapRangeColorizer : TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupColor", typeof(Color), typeof(TreeMapRangeColorizer), new PropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty RangeStopsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RangeStops", typeof(DoubleCollection), typeof(TreeMapRangeColorizer), new PropertyMetadata(OnRangeStopsChanged));

		private RangeColorizerBehavior RangeColorizerBehavior => (RangeColorizerBehavior)base.Behavior;

		public Color GroupColor
		{
			get
			{
				return (Color)GetValue(GroupColorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupColorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[TypeConverter(typeof(DoubleCollectionConverter))]
		public DoubleCollection RangeStops
		{
			get
			{
				return (DoubleCollection)GetValue(RangeStopsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(RangeStopsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnRangeStopsChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapRangeColorizer treeMapRangeColorizer)
			{
				treeMapRangeColorizer.RangeColorizerBehavior.UpdateRangeStops((DoubleCollection)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapRangeColorizer();
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return RangeColorizerBehavior.CreateLegendItems(base.Palette);
		}

		protected override LegendSupportColorizerBehavior CreateBehavior()
		{
			return new RangeColorizerBehavior();
		}

		public override Color? GetItemColor(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			if (item.IsGroup)
			{
				return GroupColor;
			}
			return RangeColorizerBehavior.GetItemColor(item.GetActualValue(), base.Palette);
		}
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapColorizerBase : TreeMapDependencyObject, ILegendItemProvider
	{
		List<ILegendItem> ILegendItemProvider.CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return CreateLegendItems(sourceItems);
		}

		protected virtual List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public abstract Color? GetItemColor(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group);
	}
	[ContentProperty("Palette")]
	public abstract class TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase : TreeMapColorizerBase, IWeakEventListener
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaletteProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Palette", typeof(PaletteBase), typeof(TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase), new PropertyMetadata(OnPaletteChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LegendItemPatternProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LegendItemPattern", typeof(string), typeof(TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase), new PropertyMetadata(OnLegendItemPatternChanged));

		protected LegendSupportColorizerBehavior Behavior { get; }

		public PaletteBase Palette
		{
			get
			{
				return (PaletteBase)GetValue(PaletteProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PaletteProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string LegendItemPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(LegendItemPatternProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LegendItemPatternProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnPaletteChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase listener)
			{
				CommonUtils.SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(e.OldValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, e.NewValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, listener);
			}
		}

		private static void OnLegendItemPatternChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase treeMapPaletteColorizerBase)
			{
				treeMapPaletteColorizerBase.Behavior.UpdateLegendItemPattern((string)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		public TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase()
		{
			SetValue(PaletteProperty, new Office2016Palette());
			Behavior = CreateBehavior();
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is PaletteBase && e is PropertyChangedEventArgs)
			{
				NotifyPropertyChanged("Colorizer");
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual LegendSupportColorizerBehavior CreateBehavior()
		{
			return new LegendSupportColorizerBehavior(WpfLegendItemCreator.Instance);
		}
	}
	public struct TreeMapItemGroupInfo
	{
		public int GroupLevel { get; private set; }

		public int GroupIndex { get; private set; }

		public int ItemIndex { get; private set; }

		public double MinValue { get; private set; }

		public double MaxValue { get; private set; }

		public TreeMapItemGroupInfo(int groupLevel, int groupIndex, int itemIndex, double minValue, double maxValue)
		{
			this = default(TreeMapItemGroupInfo);
			GroupLevel = groupLevel;
			GroupIndex = groupIndex;
			ItemIndex = itemIndex;
			MinValue = minValue;
			MaxValue = maxValue;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter : TreeMapDataAdapterBase, ISupportNativeSource, INativeItemsCollector
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataSource", typeof(object), typeof(TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(null, UpdateData));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty, UpdateData));

		private readonly TreeMapItemCollection items;

		private readonly TreeMapItemsCollector collector;

		private readonly HierarchicalDataController controller;

		public object DataSource
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(DataSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string ValueDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(ValueDataMemberProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ValueDataMemberProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected internal override TreeMapItemCollection ItemsCollection => items;

		private static void UpdateData(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter)
			{
				treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter.UpdateData();
			}
		}

		public TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter()
		{
			items = new TreeMapItemCollection();
			collector = new TreeMapItemsCollector(items);
			controller = new HierarchicalDataController(this);
		}

		IEnumerable ISupportNativeSource.GetNativeSource()
		{
			return DataSource as IEnumerable;
		}

		TreeMapItem INativeItemsCollector.ProcessAddNativeItem(object item, object parent)
		{
			double value = Convert.ToDouble(GetValue(item, ValueDataMember));
			controller.SubscribePropertiesChanged(item);
			return collector.ProcessAddNativeItem(item, parent, value);
		}

		void INativeItemsCollector.ProcessRemoveNativeItem(object item, object parentItem)
		{
			collector.ProcessRemoveNativeItem(item, parentItem);
		}

		void INativeItemsCollector.Clear()
		{
			collector.Reset();
		}

		TreeMapItemPositionInfo INativeItemsCollector.GetItemPositionInfo(TreeMapItem item)
		{
			return collector.FindPlace(item);
		}

		private void UpdateData()
		{
			collector.Reset();
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(new Action(base.UpdateItems));
		}

		private object GetValue(object item, string propertyName)
		{
			if (item != null)
			{
				Type type = item.GetType();
				PropertyInfo property = type.GetProperty(propertyName);
				if (property != null)
				{
					return property.GetValue(item, null);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter();
		}

		internal void UpdateItemValue(object nativeItem, double value)
		{
			collector.UpdateItemValue(nativeItem, value);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapFlatDataAdapter : TreeMapDataAdapterBase, IFlatDataAdapter, IGroupIndexProvider
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataSource", typeof(object), typeof(TreeMapFlatDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(null, DataSourcePropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataMember", typeof(string), typeof(TreeMapFlatDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(MemberPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ValueDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ValueDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(TreeMapFlatDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty, MemberPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LabelDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(TreeMapFlatDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty, MemberPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Groups", typeof(GroupDefinitionCollection), typeof(TreeMapFlatDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(MemberPropertyChanged));

		private readonly HierarchicalControlGroupController controller;

		private readonly TreeMapItemCollection items;

		private readonly TreeMapItemCollectionDecorator itemsDecorator;

		internal string ColorDataMember { get; set; }

		protected internal override TreeMapItemCollection ItemsCollection => items;

		public object DataSource
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(DataSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string DataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(DataMemberProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataMemberProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string ValueDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(ValueDataMemberProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ValueDataMemberProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string LabelDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(LabelDataMemberProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LabelDataMemberProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public GroupDefinitionCollection Groups
		{
			get
			{
				return (GroupDefinitionCollection)GetValue(GroupsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		string IFlatDataAdapter.ColorDataMember => ColorDataMember;

		IList<IHierarchicalItem> IFlatDataAdapter.Items => itemsDecorator;

		IEnumerable<string> IFlatDataAdapter.Groups => Groups?.GroupDataMembers;

		private static void DataSourcePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapFlatDataAdapter treeMapFlatDataAdapter)
			{
				treeMapFlatDataAdapter.controller.DataSource = e.NewValue;
				treeMapFlatDataAdapter.UpdateData();
			}
		}

		private static void MemberPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapFlatDataAdapter treeMapFlatDataAdapter)
			{
				treeMapFlatDataAdapter.UpdateData();
			}
		}

		public TreeMapFlatDataAdapter()
		{
			controller = new HierarchicalControlGroupController(this);
			items = new TreeMapItemCollection();
			itemsDecorator = new TreeMapItemCollectionDecorator(items);
			SetValue(GroupsProperty, new GroupDefinitionCollection());
		}

		private TreeMapItem CreateItem(double value, object labelObject, object tag, object color, int indexInSource)
		{
			return new TreeMapItem(value, labelObject, tag, color, indexInSource);
		}

		IHierarchicalItem IFlatDataAdapter.CreateGroupItem(int groupLevel, object labelObject, object tag, int indexInSource)
		{
			if (labelObject == null && groupLevel < Groups.Count)
			{
				labelObject = Groups[groupLevel].NullValueGroupHeader;
			}
			TreeMapItem treeMapItem = CreateItem(0.0, labelObject, tag, null, indexInSource);
			if (groupLevel < Groups.Count)
			{
				treeMapItem.HeaderContentTemplate = Groups[groupLevel].HeaderContentTemplate;
			}
			return treeMapItem;
		}

		IHierarchicalItem IFlatDataAdapter.CreateItem(double value, object labelObject, object tag, object color, int indexInSource)
		{
			return CreateItem(value, labelObject, tag, color, indexInSource);
		}

		void IFlatDataAdapter.UpdateData()
		{
			UpdateItems();
		}

		int IGroupIndexProvider.GetGroupIndex(object groupValue)
		{
			if (Groups.Count <= 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return controller.GetRowIndex(Groups[0].GroupDataMember, groupValue);
		}

		private void UpdateData()
		{
			controller.UpdateData();
			UpdateItems();
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapFlatDataAdapter();
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapGroupDefinition : TreeMapDependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupDataMemberProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupDataMember", typeof(string), typeof(TreeMapGroupDefinition), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HeaderContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(TreeMapGroupDefinition));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NullValueGroupHeaderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("NullValueGroupHeader", typeof(string), typeof(TreeMapGroupDefinition));

		public string GroupDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(GroupDataMemberProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupDataMemberProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate HeaderContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(HeaderContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(HeaderContentTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string NullValueGroupHeader
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(NullValueGroupHeaderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NullValueGroupHeaderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapGroupDefinition();
		}
	}
	public class GroupDefinitionCollection : FreezableCollection<TreeMapGroupDefinition>
	{
		protected internal IEnumerable<string> GroupDataMembers
		{
			get
			{
				using Enumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator();
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					TreeMapGroupDefinition current = enumerator.Current;
					yield return current.GroupDataMember;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Items")]
	public class TreeMapItemStorage : TreeMapDataAdapterBase
	{
		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ItemsPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("Items", typeof(TreeMapItemCollection), typeof(TreeMapItemStorage), new PropertyMetadata());

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsProperty = ItemsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;

		public TreeMapItemCollection Items => (TreeMapItemCollection)GetValue(ItemsProperty);

		protected internal override TreeMapItemCollection ItemsCollection => Items;

		public TreeMapItemStorage()
		{
			SetValue(ItemsPropertyKey, new TreeMapItemCollection());
			((INotifyCollectionChanged)Items).CollectionChanged += TreeMapItemStorage_CollectionChanged;
		}

		private void TreeMapItemStorage_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add)
			{
				SubscribeChildren(e.NewItems);
			}
			if ((e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove || e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset) && e.OldItems != null)
			{
				UnSubscribeChildren(e.OldItems);
			}
			UpdateItems();
		}

		private void UnSubscribeChildren(IList list)
		{
			foreach (object item in list)
			{
				if (item is TreeMapItem treeMapItem)
				{
					((INotifyCollectionChanged)treeMapItem.Children).CollectionChanged -= TreeMapItemStorage_CollectionChanged;
				}
			}
		}

		private void SubscribeChildren(IList list)
		{
			foreach (object item in list)
			{
				if (item is TreeMapItem treeMapItem)
				{
					((INotifyCollectionChanged)treeMapItem.Children).CollectionChanged += TreeMapItemStorage_CollectionChanged;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapItemStorage();
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalChartControlBase : Control, IPatternHolder
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolTipTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolTipTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(null));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolTipContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolTipContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(null));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolTipPatternProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolTipPattern", typeof(string), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata("{L}: {V}", OnToolTipPatternPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolTipGroupContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolTipGroupContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(null));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolTipGroupPatternProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolTipGroupPattern", typeof(string), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata("{L}: {V}", OnToolTipPatternPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolTipEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolTipEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnToolTipEnabledPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ToolTipOptionsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ToolTipOptions", typeof(ToolTipOptions), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, ToolTipOptionsPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedItemProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedItem", typeof(object), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnSelectedItemChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectedItemsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectedItems", typeof(IList), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnSelectedItemsChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SelectionModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SelectionMode", typeof(SelectionMode), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(SelectionMode.Extended));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TitlesProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Titles", typeof(TitleCollection), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LegendProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Legend", typeof(Legend), typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnLegendChanged));

		private readonly ToolTipInfo toolTipInfo;

		private readonly ViewControllerBase viewController;

		private readonly SelectedItemsControllerBase selectionController;

		private ToolTipOptions actualToolTipOptions;

		protected HitTestController HitTestController { get; }

		protected abstract ILegendItemProvider LegendItemsProvider { get; }

		protected abstract IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> SourceItemsForLegend { get; }

		internal ViewControllerBase ViewController => viewController;

		internal ToolTipOptions ActualToolTipOptions => actualToolTipOptions;

		internal bool IsSpreadsheetControl { get; set; }

		internal ILabelFormatter LabelFormatter { get; set; }

		protected internal abstract bool EnableHighlightingInternal { get; }

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public ToolTipInfo ToolTipInfo => toolTipInfo;

		[NonCategorized]
		public DataTemplate ToolTipTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(ToolTipTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolTipTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[NonCategorized]
		public DataTemplate ToolTipContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(ToolTipContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolTipContentTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[NonCategorized]
		public DataTemplate ToolTipGroupContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(ToolTipGroupContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolTipGroupContentTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		public string ToolTipPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(ToolTipPatternProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolTipPatternProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		public string ToolTipGroupPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(ToolTipGroupPatternProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolTipGroupPatternProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public bool ToolTipEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ToolTipEnabledProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolTipEnabledProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public ToolTipOptions ToolTipOptions
		{
			get
			{
				return (ToolTipOptions)GetValue(ToolTipOptionsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ToolTipOptionsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[NonCategorized]
		public object SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(SelectedItemProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedItemProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[NonCategorized]
		public IList SelectedItems
		{
			get
			{
				return (IList)GetValue(SelectedItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectedItemsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public SelectionMode SelectionMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (SelectionMode)GetValue(SelectionModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SelectionModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Elements")]
		public TitleCollection Titles
		{
			get
			{
				return (TitleCollection)GetValue(TitlesProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TitlesProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Elements")]
		public Legend Legend
		{
			get
			{
				return (Legend)GetValue(LegendProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LegendProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public event TreeMapSelectionChangedEventHandler SelectionChanged;

		private static void OnSelectedItemChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase && e.NewValue != e.OldValue)
			{
				hierarchicalChartControlBase.OnUpdateSelectedItem(e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnSelectedItemsChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase)
			{
				hierarchicalChartControlBase.OnUpdateSelectedItems(e.OldValue as IList, e.NewValue as IList);
			}
		}

		private static void OnToolTipPatternPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase)
			{
				hierarchicalChartControlBase.ViewController.HideToolTip();
			}
		}

		private static void OnToolTipEnabledPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase && !(bool)e.NewValue)
			{
				hierarchicalChartControlBase.ViewController.HideToolTip();
			}
		}

		private static void ToolTipOptionsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase)
			{
				ToolTipOptions toolTipOptions = ((e.NewValue != null) ? ((ToolTipOptions)e.NewValue) : new ToolTipOptions());
				toolTipOptions.UpdatePositionBehavior(hierarchicalChartControlBase);
				hierarchicalChartControlBase.actualToolTipOptions = toolTipOptions;
			}
		}

		private static void OnLegendChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase && e.NewValue != null)
			{
				hierarchicalChartControlBase.UpdateLegendItems();
			}
		}

		public HierarchicalChartControlBase()
		{
			SetCurrentValue(TitlesProperty, new TitleCollection());
			toolTipInfo = new ToolTipInfo(this);
			actualToolTipOptions = new ToolTipOptions();
			HitTestController = new HitTestController(this);
			selectionController = CreateSelectionController();
			viewController = CreateViewController();
			base.MouseLeftButtonUp += viewController.OnLeftButtonUp;
			base.MouseMove += viewController.OnMouseMove;
			base.MouseLeave += viewController.OnMouseLeave;
		}

		PatternDataProvider IPatternHolder.GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return GetDataProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		private void OnUpdateSelectedItem(object item)
		{
			selectionController.OnUpdateSelectedItem(item);
		}

		private void OnUpdateSelectedItems(IList oldList, IList newList)
		{
			selectionController.OnUpdateSelectedItems(oldList, newList);
		}

		protected void UpdateSelection()
		{
			selectionController.UpdateActualItemsSelection();
		}

		protected abstract PatternDataProvider GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant);

		protected abstract ViewControllerBase CreateViewController();

		protected abstract SelectedItemsControllerBase CreateSelectionController();

		protected abstract void ColorizeItems();

		protected void ColorizerChanged()
		{
			ColorizeItemsAndFillLegend();
		}

		protected void ColorizeItemsAndFillLegend()
		{
			ColorizeItems();
			if (Legend != null)
			{
				UpdateLegendItems();
			}
		}

		protected void UpdateLegendItems()
		{
			List<ILegendItem> list = LegendItemsProvider?.CreateLegendItems(SourceItemsForLegend);
			if (list != null && (Legend.Direction == LegendDirection.BottomToTop || Legend.Direction == LegendDirection.RightToLeft))
			{
				list.Reverse();
			}
			Legend.Items = list;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			InfiniteHierarchicalChartSizeException.ThrowInCaseOfInfinity(constraint, this);
			if (double.IsInfinity(constraint.Width) || double.IsInfinity(constraint.Height))
			{
				return new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}

		protected internal abstract HierarchicalControlHitInfo CalcHitInfoCore(Point point);

		internal void UpdateItemSelection(ModifierKeys keyModifiers, ISelectableItem item)
		{
			selectionController.UpdateItemSelection(SelectionMode, keyModifiers, item);
		}

		internal void ClearSelection()
		{
			selectionController.ClearSelectedItems();
		}

		internal void RaiseSelectionChanged()
		{
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null && SelectionMode != SelectionMode.None)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(this, new TreeMapSelectionChangedEventArgs(SelectedItems));
			}
		}
	}
	public class TitleCollection : ObservableCollection<Title>
	{
	}
	public class HierarchicalTreeMapItemControl : Control, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty TextProperty;

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChanged;

		private ItemType itemType = ItemType.Leaf;

		private bool hasParent;

		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(TextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[DXBrowsable(false)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public ItemType ItemType => itemType;

		event PropertyChangedEventHandler INotifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				propertyChanged = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(propertyChanged, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				propertyChanged = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(propertyChanged, value);
			}
		}

		static HierarchicalTreeMapItemControl()
		{
			TextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Text", typeof(string), typeof(HierarchicalTreeMapItemControl));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<HierarchicalTreeMapItemControl>();
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyChanged != null)
			{
				propertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		private void UpdateItemType()
		{
			itemType = (hasParent ? ItemType.SubGroup : ItemType.RootGroup);
			RaisePropertyChanged("ItemType");
		}

		private HierarchicalTreeMapItemControl FindParentHierarchicalItem()
		{
			TreeMapItemsControl treeMapItemsControl = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<TreeMapItemsControl>((DependencyObject)this);
			if (treeMapItemsControl == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return LayoutHelper.FindElementByType<HierarchicalTreeMapItemControl>((FrameworkElement)treeMapItemsControl.ParentPresentation, (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			HierarchicalTreeMapItemControl hierarchicalTreeMapItemControl = FindParentHierarchicalItem();
			if (hierarchicalTreeMapItemControl != null)
			{
				hasParent = true;
				hierarchicalTreeMapItemControl.UpdateItemType();
			}
		}
	}
	public class LeafForegroundConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(value is SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush))
			{
				return Brushes.White;
			}
			if (!CommonUtils.IsColorBright(solidColorBrush.Color))
			{
				return Brushes.White;
			}
			return Brushes.Black;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	[DXBrowsable(false)]
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[DXHelpExclude(true)]
	[NonCategorized]
	public enum ItemType
	{
		RootGroup,
		SubGroup,
		Leaf
	}
	public class HierarchicalLayoutControl : ItemsControl, IWeakEventListener
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty MasterElementProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("MasterElement", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(HierarchicalLayoutControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, MasterElementChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TitlesProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Titles", typeof(TitleCollection), typeof(HierarchicalLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(OnElementsChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LegendProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Legend", typeof(Legend), typeof(HierarchicalLayoutControl), new PropertyMetadata(OnElementsChanged));

		public FrameworkElement MasterElement
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(MasterElementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MasterElementProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public TitleCollection Titles
		{
			get
			{
				return (TitleCollection)GetValue(TitlesProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TitlesProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Legend Legend
		{
			get
			{
				return (Legend)GetValue(LegendProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LegendProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void MasterElementChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((HierarchicalLayoutControl)d).Update();
		}

		private static void OnElementsChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is HierarchicalLayoutControl hierarchicalLayoutControl)
			{
				CommonUtils.SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(e.OldValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, e.NewValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, hierarchicalLayoutControl);
				hierarchicalLayoutControl.Update();
			}
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is TitleCollection)
			{
				Update();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void Update()
		{
			List<FrameworkElement> list = new List<FrameworkElement>();
			if (MasterElement != null)
			{
				list.Add(MasterElement);
			}
			if (Titles != null)
			{
				list.AddRange(Titles);
			}
			list.Add(Legend);
			base.ItemsSource = list;
		}
	}
	public class HierarchicalLayoutControlPanel : Panel
	{
		private struct HierarchicalElementPosition : IEquatable<HierarchicalElementPosition>
		{
			public HorizontalPosition Horizontal { get; }

			public VerticalPosition Vertical { get; }

			public HierarchicalElementPosition(HorizontalPosition horizontal, VerticalPosition vertical)
			{
				Horizontal = horizontal;
				Vertical = vertical;
			}

			public bool Equals(HierarchicalElementPosition legendPosition)
			{
				if (Horizontal == legendPosition.Horizontal)
				{
					return Vertical == legendPosition.Vertical;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public override int GetHashCode()
			{
				return (int)((int)Horizontal * 10 + Vertical);
			}
		}

		private WeakReference masterElement;

		private Rect masterElementBounds;

		private FrameworkElement MasterControl
		{
			get
			{
				if (masterElement == null)
				{
					foreach (object child in base.Children)
					{
						if (child is FrameworkElement target)
						{
							masterElement = new WeakReference(target);
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				return (FrameworkElement)(masterElement?.Target);
			}
		}

		private Rect UpdateMasterElementBounds(HierarchicalLayoutElement layoutElement, Rect masterElementBounds)
		{
			Size desiredSize = layoutElement.DesiredSize;
			if (layoutElement.HorizontalPosition == HorizontalPosition.LeftOutside)
			{
				masterElementBounds.X += desiredSize.Width;
				masterElementBounds.Width -= desiredSize.Width;
			}
			else if (layoutElement.HorizontalPosition == HorizontalPosition.RightOutside)
			{
				masterElementBounds.Width -= desiredSize.Width;
			}
			if (layoutElement.VerticalPosition == VerticalPosition.TopOutside)
			{
				masterElementBounds.Y += desiredSize.Height;
				masterElementBounds.Height -= desiredSize.Height;
			}
			else if (layoutElement.VerticalPosition == VerticalPosition.BottomOutside)
			{
				masterElementBounds.Height -= desiredSize.Height;
			}
			return masterElementBounds;
		}

		private Rect UpdateSummaryCorrectionBounds(Size elementSize, Rect summaryCorrectionBounds, Dictionary<HierarchicalElementPosition, Size> sizeByPosition, HierarchicalElementPosition position)
		{
			Size size = (sizeByPosition[position] = (sizeByPosition.TryGetValue(position, out size) ? new Size(Math.Max(size.Width, elementSize.Width), size.Height + elementSize.Height) : elementSize));
			if (position.Horizontal == HorizontalPosition.LeftOutside)
			{
				summaryCorrectionBounds.X = Math.Max(summaryCorrectionBounds.X, size.Width);
			}
			else if (position.Horizontal == HorizontalPosition.RightOutside)
			{
				summaryCorrectionBounds.Width = Math.Max(summaryCorrectionBounds.Width, size.Width);
			}
			if (position.Vertical == VerticalPosition.TopOutside)
			{
				summaryCorrectionBounds.Y = Math.Max(summaryCorrectionBounds.Y, size.Height);
			}
			else if (position.Vertical == VerticalPosition.BottomOutside)
			{
				summaryCorrectionBounds.Height = Math.Max(summaryCorrectionBounds.Height, size.Height);
			}
			return summaryCorrectionBounds;
		}

		private void ArrangeElement(HierarchicalLayoutElement layoutItem, Rect availableBounds, Dictionary<HierarchicalElementPosition, double> verticalOffsetByPosition)
		{
			HierarchicalElementPosition key = new HierarchicalElementPosition(layoutItem.HorizontalPosition, layoutItem.VerticalPosition);
			double value = 0.0;
			if (!verticalOffsetByPosition.TryGetValue(key, out value))
			{
				verticalOffsetByPosition[key] = 0.0;
			}
			double height = layoutItem.DesiredSize.Height;
			verticalOffsetByPosition[key] += height;
			double y = 0.0;
			switch (key.Vertical)
			{
			case VerticalPosition.TopOutside:
				y = masterElementBounds.Top - height - value;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.Top:
				y = masterElementBounds.Top + value;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.Bottom:
				y = masterElementBounds.Bottom - value - height;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.BottomOutside:
				y = masterElementBounds.Bottom + value;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.Center:
				y = masterElementBounds.Y + masterElementBounds.Height / 2.0 - height / 2.0 + value;
				break;
			}
			double width = layoutItem.DesiredSize.Width;
			double x = 0.0;
			switch (key.Horizontal)
			{
			case HorizontalPosition.LeftOutside:
				x = availableBounds.Left;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.Left:
				x = masterElementBounds.Left;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.Right:
				x = masterElementBounds.Right - width;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.RightOutside:
				x = availableBounds.Right - width;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.Center:
				x = masterElementBounds.X + masterElementBounds.Width / 2.0 - width / 2.0;
				break;
			}
			layoutItem.Arrange(new Rect(new Point(x, y), layoutItem.DesiredSize));
		}

		private Rect ArrangeElementAndCorrectBounds(HierarchicalLayoutElement layoutItem, Rect availableBounds)
		{
			double y = 0.0;
			double height = layoutItem.DesiredSize.Height;
			switch (layoutItem.VerticalPosition)
			{
			case VerticalPosition.TopOutside:
				y = availableBounds.Top;
				availableBounds.Y += height;
				availableBounds.Height -= height;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.Top:
				y = masterElementBounds.Top;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.Bottom:
				y = masterElementBounds.Bottom - height;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.BottomOutside:
				y = availableBounds.Bottom - height;
				availableBounds.Height -= height;
				break;
			case VerticalPosition.Center:
				y = availableBounds.Y + availableBounds.Height / 2.0 - height / 2.0;
				break;
			}
			double width = layoutItem.DesiredSize.Width;
			double x = 0.0;
			switch (layoutItem.HorizontalPosition)
			{
			case HorizontalPosition.LeftOutside:
				x = availableBounds.Left;
				availableBounds.X += width;
				availableBounds.Width -= width;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.Left:
				x = masterElementBounds.Left;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.Right:
				x = masterElementBounds.Right - width;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.RightOutside:
				x = availableBounds.Right - width;
				availableBounds.Width -= width;
				break;
			case HorizontalPosition.Center:
				x = availableBounds.X + availableBounds.Width / 2.0 - width / 2.0;
				break;
			}
			layoutItem.Arrange(new Rect(new Point(x, y), layoutItem.DesiredSize));
			return availableBounds;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			Dictionary<HierarchicalElementPosition, Size> sizeByPosition = new Dictionary<HierarchicalElementPosition, Size>();
			Rect summaryCorrectionBounds = default(Rect);
			masterElementBounds = new Rect(default(Point), availableSize);
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				if (child is HierarchicalLayoutElement hierarchicalLayoutElement)
				{
					hierarchicalLayoutElement.Measure(availableSize);
					if (hierarchicalLayoutElement.ForceCorrectSize)
					{
						masterElementBounds = UpdateMasterElementBounds(hierarchicalLayoutElement, masterElementBounds);
					}
					else
					{
						summaryCorrectionBounds = UpdateSummaryCorrectionBounds(hierarchicalLayoutElement.DesiredSize, summaryCorrectionBounds, sizeByPosition, new HierarchicalElementPosition(hierarchicalLayoutElement.HorizontalPosition, hierarchicalLayoutElement.VerticalPosition));
					}
				}
			}
			masterElementBounds = new Rect(masterElementBounds.X + summaryCorrectionBounds.X, masterElementBounds.Y + summaryCorrectionBounds.Y, Math.Max(0.0, masterElementBounds.Width - summaryCorrectionBounds.X - summaryCorrectionBounds.Width), Math.Max(0.0, masterElementBounds.Height - summaryCorrectionBounds.Y - summaryCorrectionBounds.Height));
			if (MasterControl != null)
			{
				MasterControl.Measure(masterElementBounds.Size);
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			Dictionary<HierarchicalElementPosition, double> verticalOffsetByPosition = new Dictionary<HierarchicalElementPosition, double>();
			Rect availableBounds = new Rect(default(Point), finalSize);
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				if (child is HierarchicalLayoutElement hierarchicalLayoutElement)
				{
					if (hierarchicalLayoutElement.ForceCorrectSize)
					{
						availableBounds = ArrangeElementAndCorrectBounds(hierarchicalLayoutElement, availableBounds);
					}
					else
					{
						ArrangeElement(hierarchicalLayoutElement, availableBounds, verticalOffsetByPosition);
					}
				}
			}
			if (MasterControl != null)
			{
				MasterControl.Arrange(masterElementBounds);
			}
			return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalLayoutElement : Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty VerticalPositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VerticalPosition", typeof(VerticalPosition), typeof(HierarchicalLayoutElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(VerticalPosition.TopOutside, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsParentMeasure));

	public enum LegendDirection
	{
		TopToBottom,
		BottomToTop,
		LeftToRight,
		RightToLeft

	public class Legend : HierarchicalLayoutElement
	{
		[IgnoreDependencyPropertiesConsistencyChecker]
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TitleProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DirectionProperty;

		protected internal override bool ForceCorrectSize => false;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public IEnumerable<ILegendItem> Items
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<ILegendItem>)GetValue(ItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the legend title.")]
		[Category("Elements")]
		public LegendTitle Title
		{
			get
			{
				return (LegendTitle)GetValue(TitleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TitleProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets a data template that specifies how to display an item in the legend.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		public DataTemplate ItemTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(ItemTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the direction of items in the legend.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		public LegendDirection Direction
		{
			get
			{
				return (LegendDirection)GetValue(DirectionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DirectionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static bool IsReverseDirection(LegendDirection legendDirection)
		{
			if (legendDirection != LegendDirection.BottomToTop)
			{
				return legendDirection == LegendDirection.RightToLeft;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static void OnDirectionChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Legend legend = d as Legend;
			if (legend?.Items != null && (IsReverseDirection((LegendDirection)e.OldValue) ^ IsReverseDirection((LegendDirection)e.NewValue)))
			{
				legend.Items = legend.Items.Reverse();
			}
		}

		static Legend()
		{
			ItemsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Items", typeof(IEnumerable<ILegendItem>), typeof(Legend));
			TitleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Title", typeof(LegendTitle), typeof(Legend));
			ItemTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(Legend));
			DirectionProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Direction", typeof(LegendDirection), typeof(Legend), new PropertyMetadata(LegendDirection.TopToBottom, OnDirectionChanged));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<Legend>();
		}
	}

	public class LegendDirectionToOrientationConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		object IValueConverter.Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			LegendDirection legendDirection = (LegendDirection)value;
			if (legendDirection == LegendDirection.TopToBottom || legendDirection == LegendDirection.BottomToTop)
			{
				return Orientation.Vertical;
			}
			return Orientation.Horizontal;
		}

		object IValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}

	public abstract class TreeMapDataAdapterBase : TreeMapDependencyObject
	{
		protected TreeMapControl TreeMap => base.Owner as TreeMapControl;

		internal IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> ItemsForLegend { get; private set; } = new List<ILegendSourceItem>();

		protected internal abstract TreeMapItemCollection ItemsCollection { get; }

		private void UpdateItemsForLegend()
		{
			ItemsForLegend = (from item in LegendItemsCollector.CollectLastLevelGroups(ItemsCollection)
				orderby ((TreeMapItem)item).IndexInSource
				select item).Cast<ILegendSourceItem>();
		}

		protected override void OwnerChanged()
		{
			base.OwnerChanged();
			IOwnedElement itemsCollection = ItemsCollection;
			if (itemsCollection != null)
			{
				itemsCollection.Owner = TreeMap;
			}
		}

		protected internal void UpdateItems()
		{
			UpdateItemsForLegend();
			if (TreeMap != null)
			{
				TreeMap.UpdateItems();
			}
		}
	}
	public class SunburstHoleContentControl : ContentControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty HoleRadiusPercentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HoleRadiusPercent", typeof(double), typeof(SunburstHoleContentControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(20.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure));

		private Rect holeContentBounds;

		public double HoleRadiusPercent
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(HoleRadiusPercentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(HoleRadiusPercentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			if (double.IsInfinity(constraint.Width) || double.IsInfinity(constraint.Height))
			{
				return new Size(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			holeContentBounds = SunburstLayoutCalculator.CalculateHoleContentBounds(constraint, HoleRadiusPercent);
			if (VisualChildrenCount > 0)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)GetVisualChild(0);
				uIElement.Measure(holeContentBounds.Size);
			}
			return constraint;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeBounds)
		{
			if (VisualChildrenCount > 0)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)GetVisualChild(0);
				uIElement.Arrange(holeContentBounds);
			}
			return arrangeBounds;
		}
	}

	[ContentProperty("Palette")]
	public abstract class SunburstPaletteColorizerBase : SunburstColorizerBase, IWeakEventListener
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty PaletteProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Palette", typeof(PaletteBase), typeof(SunburstPaletteColorizerBase), new PropertyMetadata(OnPaletteChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LegendItemPatternProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LegendItemPattern", typeof(string), typeof(SunburstPaletteColorizerBase), new PropertyMetadata(OnLegendItemPatternChanged));

		private readonly ColorizerBrushCache brushCache = new ColorizerBrushCache();

		protected LegendSupportColorizerBehavior Behavior { get; }

		protected internal override bool CanColorize
		{
			get
			{
				PaletteBase palette = Palette;
				if (palette == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return palette.Count > 0;
			}
		}

		public PaletteBase Palette
		{
			get
			{
				return (PaletteBase)GetValue(PaletteProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PaletteProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string LegendItemPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(LegendItemPatternProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LegendItemPatternProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnPaletteChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstPaletteColorizerBase sunburstPaletteColorizerBase)
			{
				CommonUtils.SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(e.OldValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, e.NewValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, sunburstPaletteColorizerBase);
				sunburstPaletteColorizerBase.NotifyPropertyChanged("Colorizer");
			}
		}

		private static void OnLegendItemPatternChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstPaletteColorizerBase sunburstPaletteColorizerBase)
			{
				sunburstPaletteColorizerBase.Behavior.UpdateLegendItemPattern((string)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		public SunburstPaletteColorizerBase()
		{
			SetValue(PaletteProperty, new Office2016Palette());
			Behavior = CreateBehavior();
		}

		protected virtual LegendSupportColorizerBehavior CreateBehavior()
		{
			return new LegendSupportColorizerBehavior(WpfLegendItemCreator.Instance);
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is PaletteBase && e is PropertyChangedEventArgs)
			{
				OnPropertyChanged(new DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs(PaletteProperty, null, sender));
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected abstract Color GetItemColor(ISunburstSectorInfo item);

		public override Brush GetItemBrush(ISunburstSectorInfo item)
		{
			return brushCache.GetBrush(GetItemColor(item));
		}
	}
	public class SunburstPaletteColorizer : SunburstPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty VaryColorInGroupProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("VaryColorInGroup", typeof(bool), typeof(SunburstPaletteColorizer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnVaryColorInGroupChanged));

		private Color currentColor;

		public bool VaryColorInGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(VaryColorInGroupProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VaryColorInGroupProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnVaryColorInGroupChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstPaletteColorizer sunburstPaletteColorizer)
			{
				sunburstPaletteColorizer.NotifyPropertyChanged("Colorizer");
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new SunburstPaletteColorizer();
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColor(ISunburstSectorInfo item)
		{
			if (VaryColorInGroup || item.GroupInfo.GroupLevel == 0)
			{
				currentColor = base.Palette[item.GroupInfo.ItemIndex];
			}
			return currentColor;
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return base.Behavior.CreateLegendItems(sourceItems, VaryColorInGroup, WpfLegendItemCreator.CreateLegendItemBrush);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstRangeColorizer : SunburstPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty RangeStopsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("RangeStops", typeof(DoubleCollection), typeof(SunburstRangeColorizer), new PropertyMetadata(OnPropertyChanged));

		private RangeColorizerBehavior RangeColorizerBehavior => (RangeColorizerBehavior)base.Behavior;

		protected internal override bool CanColorize
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.CanColorize)
				{
					DoubleCollection rangeStops = RangeStops;
					if (rangeStops == null)
					{
						return false;
					}
					return rangeStops.Count > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		[TypeConverter(typeof(DoubleCollectionConverter))]
		public DoubleCollection RangeStops
		{
			get
			{
				return (DoubleCollection)GetValue(RangeStopsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(RangeStopsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstRangeColorizer sunburstRangeColorizer)
			{
				sunburstRangeColorizer.RangeColorizerBehavior.UpdateRangeStops((DoubleCollection)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColor(ISunburstSectorInfo item)
		{
			Color? itemColor = RangeColorizerBehavior.GetItemColor(item.Value, base.Palette);
			if (!itemColor.HasValue)
			{
				return Colors.Transparent;
			}
			return itemColor.Value;
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return RangeColorizerBehavior.CreateLegendItems(base.Palette);
		}

		protected override LegendSupportColorizerBehavior CreateBehavior()
		{
			return new RangeColorizerBehavior();
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new SunburstRangeColorizer();
		}
	}
	public class BoolToBrushConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		object IMultiValueConverter.Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(values[0] is bool))
			{
				return null;
			}
			bool flag = (bool)values[0];
			bool flag2 = (bool)values[1];
			Brush brush = (Brush)values[2];
			if ((!flag && !flag2) || !(brush is SolidColorBrush))
			{
				return brush;
			}
			Color color = ((SolidColorBrush)brush).Color;
			if (flag2)
			{
				return SelectionAndHighlightingVisualHelper.GetHighlightingBrush(color);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				return SelectionAndHighlightingVisualHelper.GetSelectionBrush(color);
			}
			return brush;
		}

		object[] IMultiValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class OpacityMaskConverter : MarkupExtension, IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}

		object IMultiValueConverter.Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values[2] is SolidColorBrush)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (values[0] is bool && (bool)values[0])
			{
				return SelectionAndHighlightingVisualHelper.GetSelectionBrush();
			}
			if (!(values[1] is bool) || !(bool)values[1])
			{
				return null;
			}
			return SelectionAndHighlightingVisualHelper.GetHighlightingBrush();
		}

		object[] IMultiValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class LabelDisplayModeToVisibilityConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if ((SunburstLabelDisplayMode)value == SunburstLabelDisplayMode.None)
			{
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			return Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}
	public abstract class SunburstDataAdapterBase : Freezable, IWeakEventListener
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataSource", typeof(object), typeof(SunburstDataAdapterBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, PropertyChanged));

		private int zeroBasedMaxLevel;

		private bool isSpreadSheetChart;

		protected List<IHierarchicalItem> HierarchicalItems { get; } = new List<IHierarchicalItem>();

		internal int LevelCount => zeroBasedMaxLevel + 1;

		internal ILabelFormatter LabelFormatter { get; set; }

		internal List<ILegendSourceItem> ItemsForLegend { get; private set; } = new List<ILegendSourceItem>();

		protected internal ObservableCollection<SunburstSectorViewInfo> SectorViewInfos { get; } = new ObservableCollection<SunburstSectorViewInfo>();

		public object DataSource
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(DataSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal event EventHandler ItemsChanged;

		protected static void PropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstDataAdapterBase sunburstDataAdapterBase)
			{
				sunburstDataAdapterBase.UpdateData();
			}
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			return ReceiveWeakEvent(sender, e);
		}

		internal void UpdateIsSpreadSheetChart(bool isSpreadSheetChart)
		{
			this.isSpreadSheetChart = isSpreadSheetChart;
		}

		private List<IHierarchicalItem> CalcGroupParameters(IList<IHierarchicalItem> hierarchicalItems, out double minValue, out double maxValue, out double sum)
		{
			sum = (minValue = (maxValue = 0.0));
			List<IHierarchicalItem> list = new List<IHierarchicalItem>();
			foreach (IHierarchicalItem hierarchicalItem in hierarchicalItems)
			{
				double value = hierarchicalItem.Value;
				if (value > 0.0)
				{
					minValue = Math.Min(minValue, value);
					maxValue = Math.Max(maxValue, value);
					sum += value;
					list.Add(hierarchicalItem);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void FillItemsForLegend(Dictionary<ILegendSourceItem, int> itemOriginalOrder)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<ILegendSourceItem, int> item in itemOriginalOrder.OrderBy((KeyValuePair<ILegendSourceItem, int> pair) => pair.Value))
			{
				ItemsForLegend.Add(item.Key);
			}
		}

		private void ClearData()
		{
			HierarchicalItems.Clear();
			SectorViewInfos.Clear();
			ItemsForLegend.Clear();
			zeroBasedMaxLevel = 0;
		}

		protected abstract bool ReceiveWeakEvent(object sender, EventArgs e);

		protected abstract void UpdateDataCore();

		protected virtual int GetLegendItemIndex(IList<IHierarchicalItem> hierarchicalItems, IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			return hierarchicalItems.IndexOf(item);
		}

		protected int CreateSectorViewInfos(IList<IHierarchicalItem> hierarchicalItems, Dictionary<ILegendSourceItem, int> itemOriginalOrder, double startAngleRad, double endAngleRad, int zeroBasedLevel, int groupIndex)
		{
			zeroBasedMaxLevel = Math.Max(zeroBasedMaxLevel, zeroBasedLevel);
			double num = startAngleRad;
			int num2 = 1;
			double minValue;
			double maxValue;
			double sum;
			List<IHierarchicalItem> source = CalcGroupParameters(hierarchicalItems, out minValue, out maxValue, out sum);
			int num3 = 0;
			foreach (IHierarchicalItem item in source.OrderByDescending((IHierarchicalItem x) => x.Value))
			{
				double num4 = Math.Abs(item.Value / sum * (endAngleRad - startAngleRad));
				double num5 = num + num4;
				bool flag = item.Children.Count > 0;
				SunburstItemGroupInfo groupInfo = new SunburstItemGroupInfo(zeroBasedLevel, groupIndex, isSpreadSheetChart ? hierarchicalItems.IndexOf(item) : num3, minValue, maxValue, flag);
				object text = item.Label;
				if (LabelFormatter != null)
				{
					text = LabelFormatter.GetDataLabelText(item.Tag, item.Label);
				}
				SunburstSectorViewInfo sunburstSectorViewInfo = new SunburstSectorViewInfo(item, num, num5, text, groupInfo, item.DataSourceColor);
				SectorViewInfos.Add(sunburstSectorViewInfo);
				if (zeroBasedLevel == 0)
				{
					itemOriginalOrder[sunburstSectorViewInfo] = GetLegendItemIndex(hierarchicalItems, item);
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					num2 += CreateSectorViewInfos(item.Children, itemOriginalOrder, num, num5, zeroBasedLevel + 1, num2 + groupIndex);
				}
				num = num5;
				num3++;
			}
			return num2;
		}

		internal void UpdateData()
		{
			ClearData();
			UpdateDataCore();
			Dictionary<ILegendSourceItem, int> itemOriginalOrder = new Dictionary<ILegendSourceItem, int>();
			CreateSectorViewInfos(HierarchicalItems, itemOriginalOrder, 0.0, Math.PI * 2.0, 0, 0);
			FillItemsForLegend(itemOriginalOrder);
			this.ItemsChanged?.Invoke(this, new EventArgs());
		}
	}
	public class SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter : SunburstDataAdapterBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty MappingsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Mappings", typeof(BindingList<SunburstDataMapping>), typeof(SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter), new PropertyMetadata(MappingsPropertyChanged));

		private readonly SunburstHierarchicalDataController controller;

		public BindingList<SunburstDataMapping> Mappings
		{
			get
			{
				return (BindingList<SunburstDataMapping>)GetValue(MappingsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MappingsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void MappingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter)
			{
				CommonUtils.SubscribeListChangedWeakEvent(e.OldValue as IBindingList, e.NewValue as IBindingList, sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter);
				sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter.UpdateData();
			}
		}

		public SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter()
		{
			SetValue(MappingsProperty, new BindingList<SunburstDataMapping>());
			controller = new SunburstHierarchicalDataController(this);
		}

		protected override bool ReceiveWeakEvent(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is BindingList<SunburstDataMapping> && e is ListChangedEventArgs)
			{
				UpdateData();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override void UpdateDataCore()
		{
			IList dataSource = MasterDetailHelper.GetDataSource(base.DataSource, (string)null);
			if (dataSource != null && dataSource.Count > 0)
			{
				controller.UpdateData(dataSource, base.HierarchicalItems, Mappings, 0, new Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos>());
			}
		}

		protected override Freezable CreateInstanceCore()
		{
			return new SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter();
		}
	}

			SetValue(GroupDataMembersProperty, new ObservableCollection<string>());
		}

		IHierarchicalItem IFlatDataAdapter.CreateGroupItem(int groupLevel, object labelObject, object tag, int indexInSource)
		{
			return new TreeMapItem(0.0, labelObject, tag, null, -1);
		}

		IHierarchicalItem IFlatDataAdapter.CreateItem(double value, object labelObject, object tag, object color, int indexInSource)
		{
			return new TreeMapItem(value, labelObject, tag, color, -1);
		}

		void IFlatDataAdapter.UpdateData()
		{
			UpdateData();
		}

		protected override bool ReceiveWeakEvent(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is ObservableCollection<string> && e is PropertyChangedEventArgs)
			{
				UpdateData();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override int GetLegendItemIndex(IList<IHierarchicalItem> hierarchicalItems, IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			ObservableCollection<string> groupDataMembers = GroupDataMembers;
			if (groupDataMembers != null && groupDataMembers.Count > 0)
			{
				return controller.GetRowIndex(GroupDataMembers[0], item.Label);
			}
			return base.GetLegendItemIndex(hierarchicalItems, item);
		}

		protected override void UpdateDataCore()
		{
			base.SectorViewInfos.Clear();
			controller.DataSource = base.DataSource;
			controller.UpdateData();
		}

		protected override Freezable CreateInstanceCore()
		{
			return new SunburstFlatDataAdapter();
		}
	}
	public class InfiniteHierarchicalChartSizeException : ApplicationException
	{
		private const string Msg = "{0} has an infinite width or height, which is not allowed. To fix the issue, place {0} into a container with the specified width and height. You can also define {0}'s Width or MaxWidth, Height, or MaxHeight.";

		internal static void ThrowInCaseOfInfinity(Size constraint, DependencyObject control)
		{
			if (DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(control) || (!double.IsInfinity(constraint.Width) && !double.IsInfinity(constraint.Height)))
			{
				return;
			}
			throw new InfiniteHierarchicalChartSizeException(string.Format("{0} has an infinite width or height, which is not allowed. To fix the issue, place {0} into a container with the specified width and height. You can also define {0}'s Width or MaxWidth, Height, or MaxHeight.", control.GetType().Name));
		}

		public InfiniteHierarchicalChartSizeException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}
	}

		protected internal override bool EnableHighlightingInternal => HighlightMode != SunburstHighlightMode.None;
