		static SunburstControl()
		{
			DataAdapterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataAdapter", typeof(SunburstDataAdapterBase), typeof(SunburstControl), new PropertyMetadata(OnDataAdapterChanged));
			ColorizerProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Colorizer", typeof(SunburstColorizerBase), typeof(SunburstControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnColorizerChanged));
			HighlightModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HighlightMode", typeof(SunburstHighlightMode), typeof(SunburstControl), new PropertyMetadata(SunburstHighlightMode.PathFromRoot));
			StartAngleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("StartAngle", typeof(double), typeof(SunburstControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnStartAngleChanged));
			SweepDirectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SweepDirection", typeof(SunburstSweepDirection), typeof(SunburstControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(SunburstSweepDirection.Clockwise, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnSweepDirectionChanged));
			HoleRadiusPercentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HoleRadiusPercent", typeof(double), typeof(SunburstControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(20.0, null, CoerceHoleRadiusPercent));
			LabelDisplayModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelDisplayMode", typeof(SunburstLabelDisplayMode), typeof(SunburstControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(SunburstLabelDisplayMode.Radial, OnLabelDisplayModePropertyChanged));
			HoleContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("HoleContent", typeof(object), typeof(SunburstControl), null);
			MinLabelSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinLabelSize", typeof(Size), typeof(SunburstControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new Size(20.0, 12.0)));
			SectorStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SectorStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(SunburstControl));
			LabelStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LabelStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(SunburstControl));
			SunburstProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("Sunburst", typeof(SunburstControl), typeof(SunburstControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits, null, null));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.UseIndependentThemeStyleKey<SunburstControl>();
		}

		private static void OnStartAngleChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SunburstControl sunburstControl = (SunburstControl)d;
			sunburstControl.Rotate((double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnSweepDirectionChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SunburstControl sunburstControl = (SunburstControl)d;
			sunburstControl.RecalculateAnglesOnSweepDirectionChanged();
		}

		private static void OnColorizerChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstControl sunburstControl)
			{
				CommonUtils.SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(e.OldValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, e.NewValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, sunburstControl);
				sunburstControl.ColorizerChanged();
			}
		}

		private static void OnDataAdapterChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.NewValue != e.OldValue)
			{
				SunburstControl sunburstControl = (SunburstControl)d;
				if (e.OldValue is SunburstDataAdapterBase sunburstDataAdapterBase)
				{
					sunburstDataAdapterBase.ItemsChanged -= sunburstControl.OnItemsChanged;
				}
				if (e.NewValue is SunburstDataAdapterBase sunburstDataAdapterBase2)
				{
					sunburstDataAdapterBase2.ItemsChanged += sunburstControl.OnItemsChanged;
					sunburstDataAdapterBase2.LabelFormatter = sunburstControl.LabelFormatter;
				}
				sunburstControl.UpdateData();
			}
		}

		private static void OnLabelDisplayModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstControl sunburstControl)
			{
				sunburstControl.UpdateLayout();
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceHoleRadiusPercent(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			double num = (double)value;
			if (num > 100.0)
			{
				return 100.0;
			}
			if (num < 0.0)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			return value;
		}

		[NonCategorized]
		[Browsable(false)]
		public static SunburstControl GetSunburst(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (SunburstControl)obj.GetValue(SunburstProperty);
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public static void SetSunburst(DependencyObject obj, SunburstControl value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(SunburstProperty, value);
		}

		public SunburstControl()
		{
			Colorizer = new SunburstPaletteColorizer();
			SetValue(SunburstProperty, this);
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (e is PropertyChangedEventArgs && sender is SunburstColorizerBase)
			{
				ColorizeItems();
				if (base.Legend != null)
				{
					UpdateLegendItems();
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void RecalculateAnglesOnSweepDirectionChanged()
		{
			if (!HasSectorViewInfos)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo in SectorViewInfos)
			{
				double startAngleRad = sectorViewInfo.StartAngleRad;
				double endAngleRad = sectorViewInfo.EndAngleRad;
				sectorViewInfo.StartAngleRad = Math.PI * 2.0 - endAngleRad;
				sectorViewInfo.EndAngleRad = Math.PI * 2.0 - startAngleRad;
			}
		}

		private void Rotate(double oldAngleDegree, double newAngleDegree)
		{
			if (!HasSectorViewInfos)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (SweepDirection == SunburstSweepDirection.Counterclockwise)
			{
				newAngleDegree = 0.0 - newAngleDegree;
				oldAngleDegree = 0.0 - oldAngleDegree;
			}
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo in SectorViewInfos)
			{
				double num = TrigonometricUtils.Degree2Radian(oldAngleDegree) - TrigonometricUtils.Degree2Radian(newAngleDegree);
				sectorViewInfo.StartAngleRad += num;
				sectorViewInfo.EndAngleRad += num;
			}
		}

		private void OnItemsChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateData();
		}

		private void UpdateData()
		{
			if (HasSectorViewInfos)
			{
				UpdateSelection();
				ColorizeItemsAndFillLegend();
				if (SweepDirection != SunburstSweepDirection.Counterclockwise)
				{
					RecalculateAnglesOnSweepDirectionChanged();
				}
				if (StartAngle != 0.0)
				{
					Rotate(0.0, StartAngle);
				}
				if (segmentItemsControl != null)
				{
					segmentItemsControl.ItemsSource = DataAdapter.SectorViewInfos;
				}
				base.ViewController.HideToolTip();
				propertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs("LevelCount"));
			}
		}

		protected override void ColorizeItems()
		{
			if (!HasSectorViewInfos)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Colorizer == null || !Colorizer.CanColorize)
			{
				foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo in DataAdapter.SectorViewInfos)
				{
					sectorViewInfo.Fill = null;
				}
				return;
			}
			Colorizer.UpdateMaxGroupLevel(DataAdapter.LevelCount);
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo2 in DataAdapter.SectorViewInfos)
			{
				sectorViewInfo2.Fill = Colorizer.GetItemBrush(sectorViewInfo2);
			}
		}

		protected override PatternDataProvider GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new SunburstItemPatternProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		protected override ViewControllerBase CreateViewController()
		{
			return new SunburstViewController(this);
		}

		protected override SelectedItemsControllerBase CreateSelectionController()
		{
			return new SunburstSelectedItemsController(this);
		}

		internal IEnumerable<SunburstSectorViewInfo> CollectRelativeItems(SunburstSectorViewInfo relativeSector, bool toZeroLevel)
		{
			if (!HasSectorViewInfos)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo item in Enumerable.Where(predicate: (!toZeroLevel) ? ((Func<SunburstSectorViewInfo, bool>)((SunburstSectorViewInfo sector) => sector.GroupInfo.GroupLevel > relativeSector.GroupInfo.GroupLevel && sector.IsInRange(relativeSector))) : ((Func<SunburstSectorViewInfo, bool>)((SunburstSectorViewInfo sector) => sector.GroupInfo.GroupLevel < relativeSector.GroupInfo.GroupLevel && relativeSector.IsInRange(sector))), source: DataAdapter.SectorViewInfos))
			{
				yield return item;
			}
		}

		protected internal override HierarchicalControlHitInfo CalcHitInfoCore(Point point)
		{
			return new SunburstHitInfo(base.HitTestController, point);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			segmentItemsControl = (SunburstSegmentItemsControl)GetTemplateChild("PART_SunburstSegmentItemsControl");
			if (segmentItemsControl != null && HasSectorViewInfos)
			{
				segmentItemsControl.ItemsSource = DataAdapter.SectorViewInfos;
			}
		}

		public SunburstHitInfo CalcHitInfo(Point point)
		{
			return (SunburstHitInfo)CalcHitInfoCore(point);
		}
	}

	public enum GradientColorizerMode
	{
		ByGroupLevel,
		ByItemIndex
	}
	public class SunburstGroupGradientColorizer : SunburstPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Min", typeof(double), typeof(SunburstGroupGradientColorizer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.5, null, CoerceMinMax));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MaxProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Max", typeof(double), typeof(SunburstGroupGradientColorizer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.9, null, CoerceMinMax));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GradientColorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GradientColor", typeof(Color), typeof(SunburstGroupGradientColorizer), new PropertyMetadata(Colors.Transparent));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Mode", typeof(GradientColorizerMode), typeof(SunburstGroupGradientColorizer), new PropertyMetadata(GradientColorizerMode.ByGroupLevel, OnModeChanged));

		private int currentGroupIndex;

		private int maxGroupLevel;

		public double Min
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MinProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MinProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double Max
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(MaxProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MaxProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Color GradientColor
		{
			get
			{
				return (Color)GetValue(GradientColorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GradientColorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public GradientColorizerMode Mode
		{
			get
			{
				return (GradientColorizerMode)GetValue(ModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceMinMax(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 1.0;
			double num3 = (double)value;
			if (num3 > num2)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			if (num3 < num)
			{
				return num;
			}
			return value;
		}

		private static void OnModeChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is SunburstGroupGradientColorizer sunburstGroupGradientColorizer)
			{
				sunburstGroupGradientColorizer.NotifyPropertyChanged("Colorizer");
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new SunburstGroupGradientColorizer();
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColor(ISunburstSectorInfo item)
		{
			SunburstItemGroupInfo groupInfo = item.GroupInfo;
			double ratio;
			if (Mode == GradientColorizerMode.ByGroupLevel)
			{
				if (groupInfo.GroupLevel == 0)
				{
					currentGroupIndex = groupInfo.ItemIndex;
				}
				ratio = Min + (double)(maxGroupLevel - groupInfo.GroupLevel) * ((Max - Min) / (double)maxGroupLevel);
				return ColorizerCalculator.MixColors(GradientColor, base.Palette[currentGroupIndex], ratio);
			}
			ratio = ((groupInfo.MaxValue > groupInfo.MinValue) ? (Max - (groupInfo.MaxValue - item.Value) * ((Max - Min) / (groupInfo.MaxValue - groupInfo.MinValue))) : Max);
			return ColorizerCalculator.MixColors(GradientColor, base.Palette[groupInfo.GroupIndex], ratio);
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return base.Behavior.CreateLegendItems(sourceItems, Mode == GradientColorizerMode.ByItemIndex, (ILegendSourceItem item) => WpfLegendItemCreator.CreateLegendItemGradientBrush(item, GradientColor));
		}

		internal override void UpdateMaxGroupLevel(int maxGroupLevel)
		{
			this.maxGroupLevel = maxGroupLevel;
		}
	}

	[NonCategorized]
	public class ToolTipPanel : Panel
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty PositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Position", typeof(Point), typeof(ToolTipPanel), new PropertyMetadata(InvalidateLayout));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShadowPaddingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ShadowPadding", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ToolTipPanel), new PropertyMetadata(InvalidateLayout), ValidateShadowPadding);

		private HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl;

		private Popup popup;

		public Point Position
		{
			get
			{
				return (Point)GetValue(PositionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PositionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private HierarchicalChartControlBase HierarchicalControl
		{
			get
			{
				if (hierarchicalControl == null)
				{
					hierarchicalControl = LayoutHelper.FindLayoutOrVisualParentObject((DependencyObject)this, typeof(HierarchicalChartControlBase), false, (DependencyObject)null) as HierarchicalChartControlBase;
				}
				return hierarchicalControl;
			}
		}

		private Popup Popup
		{
			get
			{
				if (popup == null)
				{
					popup = FindPopup();
				}
				return popup;
			}
		}

		private static void InvalidateLayout(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is ToolTipPanel toolTipPanel)
			{
				toolTipPanel.InvalidateMeasure();
			}
		}

		private static bool ValidateShadowPadding(object value)
		{
			Thickness thickness = (Thickness)value;
			if (thickness.Left >= 0.0 && thickness.Top >= 0.0 && thickness.Bottom >= 0.0)
			{
				return thickness.Right >= 0.0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static Thickness GetShadowPadding(DependencyObject target)
		{
			return (Thickness)target.GetValue(ShadowPaddingProperty);
		}

		public static void SetShadowPadding(DependencyObject target, Thickness value)
		{
			target.SetValue(ShadowPaddingProperty, value);
		}

		private Popup FindPopup()
		{
			return base.Children[0] as Popup;
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (Popup != null && Popup.Child != null)
			{
				Popup.Child.Measure(new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			if (HierarchicalControl != null && Popup != null && Popup.Child != null)
			{
				UIElement child = Popup.Child;
				child.InvalidateArrange();
				Size desiredSize = child.DesiredSize;
				child.Arrange(new Rect(new Point(0.0, 0.0), desiredSize));
				ToolTipInfo toolTipInfo = HierarchicalControl.ToolTipInfo;
				if (toolTipInfo != null && toolTipInfo.Layout != null)
				{
					ToolTipLayoutStrategy toolTipLayoutStrategy = ToolTipLayoutStrategy.Create(base.FlowDirection);
					Size toolTipSize = ToolTipLayoutHelper.CorrectSizeByTransform(desiredSize, HierarchicalControl);
					Point anchorPoint = toolTipLayoutStrategy.CalculateToolTipPosition(Position, toolTipSize, toolTipInfo.Layout.ScreenBounds);
					toolTipInfo.HorizontalBeakAlignment = toolTipLayoutStrategy.CalculateHorizontalBeakPosition(Position, toolTipSize, toolTipInfo.Layout.ScreenBounds);
					toolTipInfo.VerticalBeakAlignment = toolTipLayoutStrategy.CalculateVerticalBeakPosition(Position, toolTipSize, toolTipInfo.Layout.ScreenBounds);
					if (VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(popup.Child) > 0)
					{
						DependencyObject child2 = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(popup.Child, 0);
						Thickness shadowPadding = GetShadowPadding(child2);
						anchorPoint = toolTipLayoutStrategy.CorrectPositionByShadow(anchorPoint, HierarchicalControl, toolTipInfo.VerticalBeakAlignment, toolTipInfo.HorizontalBeakAlignment, shadowPadding);
					}
					Popup.HorizontalOffset = anchorPoint.X;
					Popup.VerticalOffset = anchorPoint.Y;
				}
			}
			return base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
		}
	}
	[NonCategorized]

	public abstract class GenericEnumToVisibilityConverter<TEnum> : IValueConverter where TEnum : struct
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (targetType == typeof(Visibility) && parameter != null && Enum.TryParse<TEnum>(parameter.ToString(), out var result) && value is TEnum)
			{
				return (!result.Equals((TEnum)value)) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class HorizontalBeakAlignmentToVisibilityConverter : GenericEnumToVisibilityConverter<HorizontalAlignment>
	{
	}
	public class VerticalBeakAlignmentToVisibilityConverter : GenericEnumToVisibilityConverter<VerticalAlignment>
	{
	}
	public enum ToolTipOpenMode
	{

	}
	public class ToolTipOptions : TreeMapDependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OpenModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("OpenMode", typeof(ToolTipOpenMode), typeof(ToolTipOptions), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ToolTipOpenMode.OnClick));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Position", typeof(ToolTipPosition), typeof(ToolTipOptions), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, PositionPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty InitialDelayProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("InitialDelay", typeof(TimeSpan), typeof(ToolTipOptions), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 1)));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AutoPopDelayProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AutoPopDelay", typeof(TimeSpan), typeof(ToolTipOptions), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(TimeSpan.Zero));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CloseOnClickProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CloseOnClick", typeof(bool), typeof(ToolTipOptions), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public ToolTipOpenMode OpenMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (ToolTipOpenMode)GetValue(OpenModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OpenModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Layout")]
		public ToolTipPosition Position
		{
			get
			{
				return (ToolTipPosition)GetValue(PositionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PositionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public TimeSpan InitialDelay
		{
			get
			{
				return (TimeSpan)GetValue(InitialDelayProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(InitialDelayProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public TimeSpan AutoPopDelay
		{
			get
			{
				return (TimeSpan)GetValue(AutoPopDelayProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AutoPopDelayProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public bool CloseOnClick
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(CloseOnClickProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CloseOnClickProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal ToolTipPosition ActualPosition { get; private set; }

		private static void PositionPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is ToolTipOptions toolTipOptions)
			{
				if (e.NewValue is ToolTipPosition toolTipPosition)
				{
					toolTipOptions.ActualPosition = toolTipPosition;
					toolTipPosition.UpdateBehavior(toolTipOptions.Owner);
				}
				else
				{
					toolTipOptions.ActualPosition = new ToolTipMousePosition();
				}
			}
		}

		public ToolTipOptions()
		{
			ActualPosition = new ToolTipMousePosition();
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new ToolTipOptions();
		}

		internal void UpdatePositionBehavior(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
		{
			ActualPosition.UpdateBehavior(hierarchicalControl);
		}
	}
	public abstract class ToolTipPosition : TreeMapDependencyObject
	{
		protected internal abstract Point CalculateToolTipPoint(Point mousePosition, FrameworkElement itemPresentation, HierarchicalChartControlBase target);

		protected internal virtual void UpdateBehavior(object owner)
		{
		}
	}
	public class ToolTipMousePosition : ToolTipPosition
	{
		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new ToolTipMousePosition();
		}

		protected internal override Point CalculateToolTipPoint(Point mousePosition, FrameworkElement itemPresentation, HierarchicalChartControlBase target)
		{
			return mousePosition;
		}
	}
	public class ToolTipRelativePosition : ToolTipPosition
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OriginProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Origin", typeof(Point), typeof(ToolTipRelativePosition), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new Point(0.5, 0.5)), ValidateOrigin);

		private ToolTipRelativePositionBase behavior;

		[Category("Layout")]
		public Point Origin
		{
			get
			{
				return (Point)GetValue(OriginProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OriginProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static bool ValidateOrigin(object value)
		{
			Point point = (Point)value;
			if (point.X <= 1.0 && point.X >= 0.0 && point.Y <= 1.0)
			{
				return point.Y >= 0.0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new ToolTipRelativePosition();
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateBehavior(object owner)
		{
			if (owner is TreeMapControl)
			{
				behavior = new TreeMapToolTipRelativePosition();
			}
			else
			{
				behavior = new SunburstToolTipRelativePosition();
			}
		}

		protected internal override Point CalculateToolTipPoint(Point mousePosition, FrameworkElement itemPresentation, HierarchicalChartControlBase target)
		{
			Rect relativeElementRect = LayoutHelper.GetRelativeElementRect((UIElement)itemPresentation, (UIElement)target);
			return behavior.CalculateToolTipPoint(relativeElementRect, itemPresentation, Origin);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemsControl : ItemsControl
	{
		private static readonly Thickness DefaultBorderThickness;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ParentItemProperty;

		private TreeMapItemPresentation parentPresentation;

		private int groupLevel;

		private INativeItemsCollector Collector => Interaction?.Collector;

		internal InteractionData Interaction { get; set; }

		internal TreeMapItemPresentation ParentPresentation => parentPresentation;

		[DXBrowsable(false)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public int GroupLevel => groupLevel;

		public object ParentItem
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ParentItemProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ParentItemProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static TreeMapItemsControl()
		{
			DefaultBorderThickness = new Thickness(1.0);
			ParentItemProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ParentItem", typeof(object), typeof(TreeMapItemsControl));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<TreeMapItemsControl>();
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			if (!(element is TreeMapItemPresentation treeMapItemPresentation))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (item is TreeMapItem treeMapItem)
			{
				treeMapItemPresentation.TreeMapItem = treeMapItem;
				treeMapItemPresentation.ItemsSource = treeMapItem.Children;
				treeMapItem.UpdateTemplate();
				if (ParentItem is TreeMapItem treeMapItem2)
				{
					treeMapItem2.UpdateTemplate();
				}
			}
			else if (Collector != null)
			{
				treeMapItemPresentation.TreeMapItem = Collector.ProcessAddNativeItem(item, ParentItem);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnItemsChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnItemsChanged(e);
			if (Interaction == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e.OldItems != null)
			{
				foreach (object oldItem in e.OldItems)
				{
					Collector.ProcessRemoveNativeItem(oldItem, ParentItem);
				}
			}
			Interaction.Invalidate();
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			return new TreeMapItemPresentation();
		}

		protected override bool IsItemItsOwnContainerOverride(object item)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (Interaction != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject parent = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(this);
			if (parent == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			parentPresentation = LayoutHelper.FindAmongParents<TreeMapItemPresentation>(parent, (DependencyObject)null);
			TreeMapItemsControl treeMapItemsControl = LayoutHelper.FindAmongParents<TreeMapItemsControl>(parent, (DependencyObject)null);
			if (treeMapItemsControl != null)
			{
				if (treeMapItemsControl.ParentPresentation != null)
				{
					treeMapItemsControl.ParentPresentation.BorderThickness = DefaultBorderThickness;
				}
				groupLevel = treeMapItemsControl.GroupLevel + 1;
				Interaction = treeMapItemsControl.Interaction;
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapDependencyObject : Freezable, IOwnedElement, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private object owner;

		public object Owner => owner;

		object IOwnedElement.Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return owner;
			}
			set
			{
				if (owner != value)
				{
					owner = value;
					OwnerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		protected bool SetProperty<T>(ref T storage, T value, [CallerMemberName] string propertyName = null)
		{
			if (object.Equals(storage, value))
			{
				return false;
			}
			storage = value;
			NotifyPropertyChanged(propertyName);
			return true;
		}

		protected void NotifyPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}

		protected abstract TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject();

		protected virtual void OwnerChanged()
		{
		}

		protected override Freezable CreateInstanceCore()
		{
			return CreateObject();
		}
	}

		IList<IHierarchicalItem> IHierarchicalItem.Children => childrenDecorator;

		object IHierarchicalItem.DataSourceColor => Color;

		object IToolTipItem.SourceObject => Tag;

		bool IToolTipItem.IsGroup => children.Count > 0;

		string ILegendSourceItem.Text => labelString;

		object ILegendSourceItem.Brush => Children.OrderByDescending((TreeMapItem x) => x.Value).First().ActualBackground;

		object ILegendSourceItem.SourceObject => this;

		internal TreeMapItem(double value, object label, object tag, object color, int indexInSource)
			: this()
		{
			IndexInSource = indexInSource;
			this.value = value;
			this.label = label;
			labelString = ObtainLabelString(label);
			this.tag = tag;
			Color = color;
		}

		public TreeMapItem()
		{
			childrenDecorator = new TreeMapItemCollectionDecorator(Children);
		}

		public TreeMapItem(double value, string label)
			: this(value, label, null, null, -1)
		{
		}

		void IHierarchicalItem.NotifyChildrenUpdate()
		{
			TreeMapControl treeMap = TreeMap;
			if (treeMap == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in treeMap.GetItemPath(this))
			{
				item.NotifyPropertyChanged("Children");
			}
		}

		protected override void OwnerChanged()
		{
			((IOwnedElement)Children).Owner = base.Owner;
			labelString = ObtainLabelString(label);
		}

		PatternDataProvider IPatternHolder.GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new TreeMapItemPatternProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		List<object> ISelectableItem.GetObjectsToSelect(SelectedItemsControllerBase selectionController)
		{
			return GetSelectedItems(this);
		}

		private Brush CalculateActualBackground(TreeMapStyle style)
		{
			if (IsGroup)
			{
				if (groupLevel <= 0)
				{
					return style.ActualGroupBackground;
				}
				return style.ActualSubGroupBackground;
			}
			return Brushes.Transparent;
		}

		private Brush CalculateActualForeground(TreeMapStyle style)
		{
			if (IsGroup)
			{
				if (groupLevel <= 0)
				{
					return style.ActualGroupForeground;
				}
				return style.ActualSubGroupForeground;
			}
			object obj = style.LeafForeground;
			if (obj == null)
			{
				if (!CommonUtils.IsColorBright(((SolidColorBrush)ActualBackground).Color))
				{
					return Brushes.White;
				}
				obj = Brushes.Black;
			}
			return (Brush)obj;
		}

		private string ObtainLabelString(object label)
		{
			ILabelFormatter labelFormatter = TreeMap?.LabelFormatter;
			if (labelFormatter != null)
			{
				return labelFormatter.GetDataLabelText(tag, label);
			}
			if (label == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			PatternParser patternParser = TreeMapStyle?.PatternParser;
			if (patternParser == null)
			{
				return label.ToString();
			}
			patternParser.SetContext(this);
			return patternParser.GetText();
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new TreeMapItem();
		}

		internal void UpdateGroupLevel(int level)
		{
			if (groupLevel != level)
			{
				groupLevel = level;
				ApplyAppearance();
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			ActualTemplate = (IsGroup ? GroupHeaderContentTemplate : LeafContentTemplate);
			ApplyAppearance();
		}

		protected internal double GetActualValue()
		{
			if (Value > 0.0 || !IsGroup)
			{
				return Value;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (TreeMapItem child in Children)
			{
				num += child.GetActualValue();
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected internal virtual void ApplyAppearance()
		{
			TreeMapStyle treeMapStyle = TreeMapStyle;
			if (treeMapStyle == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ActualBackground = ((ColorizerBrush != null) ? ColorizerBrush : ((Background != null) ? Background : CalculateActualBackground(treeMapStyle)));
			ActualForeground = Foreground ?? CalculateActualForeground(treeMapStyle);
			GroupBorderBrush = treeMapStyle.ActualGroupBorderBrush;
			BorderThickness = treeMapStyle.BorderThickness;
			BorderBrush = treeMapStyle.BorderBrush;
			LeafTextHorizontalAlignment = treeMapStyle.LeafTextHorizontalAlignment;
			LeafTextVerticalAlignment = treeMapStyle.LeafTextVerticalAlignment;
			LabelVisible = (IsGroup ? treeMapStyle.GroupLabelVisible : treeMapStyle.LeafLabelVisible);
			if (!LabelVisible)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (IsGroup)
			{
				double num = ((groupLevel > 0) ? treeMapStyle.ActualSubGroupFontSize : treeMapStyle.ActualGroupFontSize);
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					FontSize = num;
				}
				if (treeMapStyle.ActualGroupFontFamily != null)
				{
					FontFamily = treeMapStyle.ActualGroupFontFamily;
				}
				FontWeight = ((groupLevel > 0) ? treeMapStyle.ActualSubGroupFontWeight : treeMapStyle.ActualGroupFontWeight);
				FontStyle = treeMapStyle.ActualGroupFontStyle;
			}
			else
			{
				if (treeMapStyle.ActualLeafFontSize > 0.0)
				{
					FontSize = treeMapStyle.ActualLeafFontSize;
				}
				if (treeMapStyle.ActualLeafFontFamily != null)
				{
					FontFamily = treeMapStyle.ActualLeafFontFamily;
				}
				FontWeight = treeMapStyle.ActualLeafFontWeight;
				FontStyle = treeMapStyle.ActualLeafFontStyle;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void ApplyOpacityMask()
		{
			OpacityMask = (IsSelected ? SelectionAndHighlightingVisualHelper.GetSelectionBrush() : (IsHighlighted ? SelectionAndHighlightingVisualHelper.GetHighlightingBrush() : null));
		}

		private List<object> FillSelectedTreeMapItems(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> children)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			foreach (IHierarchicalItem child in children)
			{
				list.AddRange(GetSelectedItems(child));
			}
			return list;
		}

		private List<object> GetSelectedItems(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			if (TreeMapItemHelper.IsGroupItem(item))
			{
				list.AddRange(FillSelectedTreeMapItems(item.Children));
			}
			else
			{
				list.Add(item.Tag ?? item);
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemPresentation : HeaderedItemsControl, IHitTestableElement

			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<TreeMapItemPresentation>();

	public class ListToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (!(value is IList) || ((IList)value).Count <= 0) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class BoolToVisibilityConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (!(value is bool) || !(bool)value) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemPanel : Panel
	{
		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				if (child.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					child.Measure(new Size(availableSize.Width, Math.Max(availableSize.Height - num, 0.0)));
					num += child.DesiredSize.Height;
				}
			}
			return new Size(availableSize.Width, num);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				if (child.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					list.Add(child);
				}
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count - 1; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement2 = list[i];
				uIElement2.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, num, finalSize.Width, uIElement2.DesiredSize.Height));
				num += uIElement2.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				list[list.Count - 1].Arrange(new Rect(0.0, num, finalSize.Width, Math.Max(finalSize.Height - num, 0.0)));
			}
			return finalSize;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapTextPanel : Grid
	{
		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				child.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				child.Measure(new Size(availableSize.Width, availableSize.Height));
				child.Visibility = ((child.DesiredSize.Height >= availableSize.Height) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemCollection : FreezableCollection<TreeMapItem>, IOwnedElement
	{
		private object owner;

		protected object Owner => owner;

		protected TreeMapControl TreeMap => Owner as TreeMapControl;

		object IOwnedElement.Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return owner;
			}
			set
			{
				RemoveChildren(this);
				owner = value;
				AddChildren(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void AddChildren(IList children)
		{
			SetOwnerForChildren(children, owner);
		}

		protected virtual void RemoveChildren(IList children)
		{
			SetOwnerForChildren(children, null);
		}

		private void SetOwnerForChildren(IList children, object owner)
		{
			foreach (object child in children)
			{
				if (child is IOwnedElement ownedElement)
				{
					ownedElement.Owner = owner;
				}
			}
		}

		public TreeMapItemCollection()
		{
			((INotifyCollectionChanged)this).CollectionChanged += OnCollectionChanged;
		}

		private void OnCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OldItems != null && (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset || e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Remove || e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace))
			{
				RemoveChildren(e.OldItems);
			}
			if (e.NewItems != null && (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add || e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Replace))
			{
				AddChildren(e.NewItems);
			}
		}
	}
	public enum LayoutDirection
	{
		TopLeftToBottomRight,
		BottomLeftToTopRight,
		TopRightToBottomLeft,
		BottomRightToTopLeft
	}
	public enum SliceAndDiceLayoutMode
	{
		Auto,
		Vertical,
		Horizontal
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase : TreeMapDependencyObject
	{
		public abstract void Calculate(IList<ITreeMapLayoutItem> items, Size size, int groupLevel);
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapLayoutAlgorithm : TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DirectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Direction", typeof(LayoutDirection), typeof(TreeMapLayoutAlgorithm), new PropertyMetadata(LayoutDirection.TopLeftToBottomRight));

		public LayoutDirection Direction
		{
			get
			{
				return (LayoutDirection)GetValue(DirectionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DirectionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected IList<TreeMapItemLayout> CreateSortedItemsList(IList<ITreeMapLayoutItem> items)
		{
			List<TreeMapItemLayout> list = new List<TreeMapItemLayout>();
			foreach (ITreeMapLayoutItem item in items)
			{
				list.Add(new TreeMapItemLayout(item));
			}
			list.Sort(new TreeMapLayoutItemComparer());
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class SquarifiedLayoutAlgorithm : TreeMapLayoutAlgorithm
	{
		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new SquarifiedLayoutAlgorithm();
		}

		public override void Calculate(IList<ITreeMapLayoutItem> items, Size size, int groupLevel)
		{
			new SquarifiedAlgorithm((LayoutAlgorithmDirection)base.Direction).Calculate(CreateSortedItemsList(items), size.Width, size.Height);
		}
	}
	public class StripedLayoutAlgorithm : TreeMapLayoutAlgorithm
	{
		private const double defaultLastStripeMinThickness = 0.025;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LastStripeMinThicknessProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LastStripeMinThickness", typeof(double), typeof(StripedLayoutAlgorithm), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.025, null, CoerceLastStripeMinThickness));

		public double LastStripeMinThickness
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(LastStripeMinThicknessProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LastStripeMinThicknessProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static object CoerceLastStripeMinThickness(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 1.0;
			double num3 = (double)value;
			if (num3 > num2)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			if (num3 < num)
			{
				return num;
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new StripedLayoutAlgorithm();
		}

		public override void Calculate(IList<ITreeMapLayoutItem> items, Size size, int groupLevel)
		{
			new StripedAlgorithm((LayoutAlgorithmDirection)base.Direction, LastStripeMinThickness).Calculate(CreateSortedItemsList(items), size.Width, size.Height);
		}
	}
	public class SliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm : TreeMapLayoutAlgorithm
	{
		private Dictionary<int, SliceAndDiceLayoutMode> groupLevelsDirections = new Dictionary<int, SliceAndDiceLayoutMode>();

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LayoutModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LayoutMode", typeof(SliceAndDiceLayoutMode), typeof(SliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm), new PropertyMetadata(SliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Auto));

		public SliceAndDiceLayoutMode LayoutMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (SliceAndDiceLayoutMode)GetValue(LayoutModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LayoutModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private SliceAndDiceLayoutMode GetActualLayoutMode(double width, double height, int groupLevel)
		{
			if (groupLevel == 0)
			{
				CalculateMode(width, height);
				return LayoutMode;
			}
			return groupLevelsDirections[groupLevel % 2];
		}

		private void CalculateMode(double width, double height)
		{
			if (LayoutMode == SliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Auto)
			{
				groupLevelsDirections[0] = ((width > height) ? SliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Vertical : SliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Horizontal);
			}
			else
			{
				groupLevelsDirections[0] = LayoutMode;
			}
			groupLevelsDirections[1] = ((groupLevelsDirections[0] == SliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Horizontal) ? SliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Vertical : SliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Horizontal);
		}

		protected override TreeMapDependencyObject CreateObject()
		{
			return new SliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm();
		}

		public override void Calculate(IList<ITreeMapLayoutItem> items, Size size, int groupLevel)
		{
			SliceAndDiceLayoutMode actualLayoutMode = GetActualLayoutMode(size.Width, size.Height, groupLevel);
			new SliceAndDiceAlgorithm((LayoutAlgorithmDirection)base.Direction, (SliceAndDiceAlgorithmLayoutMode)actualLayoutMode).Calculate(CreateSortedItemsList(items), size.Width, size.Height);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapPanel : Panel
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupLevelProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupLevel", typeof(int), typeof(TreeMapPanel), new PropertyMetadata(GroupLevelPropertyChanged));

		private const double defaultWidth = 300.0;

		private const double defaultHeight = 300.0;

		private WeakReference interaction;

		[DXBrowsable(false)]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public int GroupLevel
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(GroupLevelProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupLevelProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private InteractionData Interaction
		{
			get
			{
				if (interaction == null)
				{
					TreeMapItemsControl treeMapItemsControl = LayoutHelper.FindAmongParents<TreeMapItemsControl>((DependencyObject)this, (DependencyObject)null);
					if (treeMapItemsControl != null)
					{
						interaction = new WeakReference(treeMapItemsControl.Interaction);
					}
				}
				return (InteractionData)(interaction?.Target);
			}
		}

		private ITreeMapLayoutCalculator LayoutCalculator => Interaction?.LayoutCalculator;

		private ITreeMapItemColorizer TreeMapItemColorizer => Interaction?.TreeMapItemColorizer;

		private static void GroupLevelPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapPanel treeMapPanel)
			{
				treeMapPanel.UpdateItemsGroupLevel();
			}
		}

		private bool ItemSizeCorrect(ITreeMapLayoutItem item, Size finalSize)
		{
			if (Math.Round(finalSize.Width - item.Layout.Width, 3) >= 0.0)
			{
				return Math.Round(finalSize.Height - item.Layout.Height, 3) >= 0.0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void UpdateItemsGroupLevel()
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				if (child is TreeMapItemPresentation { TreeMapItem: not null } treeMapItemPresentation)
				{
					treeMapItemPresentation.TreeMapItem.UpdateGroupLevel(GroupLevel);
				}
			}
		}

		private double CalculateSum(List<TreeMapItemPresentation> presentations)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (TreeMapItemPresentation presentation in presentations)
			{
				num += presentation.LayoutItem.Weight;
			}
			return num;
		}

		private void CalculateLayoutAndMeasureItems(Size constraint, List<TreeMapItemPresentation> list, List<ITreeMapLayoutItem> items)
		{
			if (LayoutCalculator != null)
			{
				LayoutCalculator.CalculateLayout(items, constraint, GroupLevel);
			}
			foreach (TreeMapItemPresentation item in list)
			{
				Size size = new Size(0.0, 0.0);
				size = TreeMapPanelLayoutHelper.RoundChildLayout(item.LayoutItem.Layout, constraint).Size;
				item.Measure(size);
				if (item.TreeMapItem.Children.Count > 0)
				{
					UIElementExtensions.InvalidateParentsOfModifiedChildren((UIElement)item);
				}
				if (TreeMapItemColorizer != null)
				{
					TreeMapItemColorizer.TryColorizeTreeMapItem(item.TreeMapItem);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			double width = (double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width) ? 300.0 : availableSize.Width);
			double height = (double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Height) ? 300.0 : availableSize.Height);
			Size size = new Size(width, height);
			List<TreeMapItemPresentation> list = new List<TreeMapItemPresentation>();
			List<ITreeMapLayoutItem> list2 = new List<ITreeMapLayoutItem>();
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				if (child is TreeMapItemPresentation { LayoutItem: not null } treeMapItemPresentation)
				{
					list2.Add(treeMapItemPresentation.LayoutItem);
					list.Add(treeMapItemPresentation);
				}
			}
			double num = CalculateSum(list);
			CalculateLayoutAndMeasureItems(size, list, list2);
			if (GroupLevel == 0 && num != CalculateSum(list))
			{
				CalculateLayoutAndMeasureItems(size, list, list2);
			}
			return size;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				Rect finalRect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				if (child is TreeMapItemPresentation { LayoutItem: not null } treeMapItemPresentation && ItemSizeCorrect(treeMapItemPresentation.LayoutItem, finalSize))
				{
					finalRect = TreeMapPanelLayoutHelper.RoundChildLayout(treeMapItemPresentation.LayoutItem.Layout, finalSize);
				}
				child.Arrange(finalRect);
			}
			return finalSize;
		}
	}
	[DXToolboxBrowsable]
	[ContentProperty("DataAdapter")]
	public class TreeMapControl : HierarchicalChartControlBase, ITreeMapLayoutCalculator, INativeItemsCollector, ITreeMapItemColorizer, IWeakEventListener
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty LayoutAlgorithmProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DataAdapterProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ColorizerProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupHeaderContentTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LeafContentTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TreeMapStyleProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty EnableHighlightingProperty;

		private ColorizerController colorizerController;

		private TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase actualLayoutAlgorithm;

		private TreeMapPanel panel;

		private WeakReference itemsContainer;

		private ISupportNativeSource nativeDataSource;

		private bool isUpdatesLocked;

		protected internal override bool EnableHighlightingInternal => EnableHighlighting;

		[Category("Data")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		public TreeMapDataAdapterBase DataAdapter
		{
			get
			{
				return (TreeMapDataAdapterBase)GetValue(DataAdapterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DataAdapterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Layout")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		public TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase LayoutAlgorithm
		{
			get
			{
				return (TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase)GetValue(LayoutAlgorithmProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LayoutAlgorithmProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		public TreeMapColorizerBase Colorizer
		{
			get
			{
				return (TreeMapColorizerBase)GetValue(ColorizerProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ColorizerProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[NonCategorized]
		public DataTemplate GroupHeaderContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(GroupHeaderContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupHeaderContentTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[NonCategorized]
		public DataTemplate LeafContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(LeafContentTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LeafContentTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		public TreeMapStyle TreeMapStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return (TreeMapStyle)GetValue(TreeMapStyleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TreeMapStyleProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[Category("Behavior")]
		public bool EnableHighlighting
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(EnableHighlightingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(EnableHighlightingProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase ActualLayoutAlgorithm
		{
			get
			{
				return actualLayoutAlgorithm;
			}
			set
			{
				actualLayoutAlgorithm = value ?? new SquarifiedLayoutAlgorithm();
				InvalidateLayout();
			}
		}

		private TreeMapItemsControl ItemsContainer => (TreeMapItemsControl)(itemsContainer?.Target);

		private TreeMapPanel Panel
		{
			get
			{
				if (panel == null && ItemsContainer != null)
				{
					panel = LayoutHelper.FindElementByType<TreeMapPanel>((FrameworkElement)ItemsContainer, (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null);
				}
				return panel;
			}
		}

		private ColorizerController ColorizerController
		{
			get
			{
				if (colorizerController == null)
				{
					colorizerController = (base.IsSpreadsheetControl ? new SpreadsheetColorizerController(this) : new ColorizerController(this));
				}
				return colorizerController;
			}
		}

		protected override ILegendItemProvider LegendItemsProvider => Colorizer;

		protected override IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> SourceItemsForLegend => DataAdapter?.ItemsForLegend;

		internal TreeMapItemCollection ActualItems => DataAdapter?.ItemsCollection;

		internal IGroupIndexProvider GroupIndexProvider => DataAdapter as IGroupIndexProvider;

		static TreeMapControl()
		{
			LayoutAlgorithmProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LayoutAlgorithm", typeof(TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase), typeof(TreeMapControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, LayoutAlgorithmPropertyChanged));
			DataAdapterProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DataAdapter", typeof(TreeMapDataAdapterBase), typeof(TreeMapControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, DataAdapterPropertyChanged));
			ColorizerProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Colorizer", typeof(TreeMapColorizerBase), typeof(TreeMapControl), new PropertyMetadata(ColorizerPropertyChanged));
			GroupHeaderContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("GroupHeaderContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(TreeMapControl), new PropertyMetadata(TemplateChanged));
			LeafContentTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LeafContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(TreeMapControl), new PropertyMetadata(TemplateChanged));
			TreeMapStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TreeMapStyle", typeof(TreeMapStyle), typeof(TreeMapControl), new PropertyMetadata(TreeMapStylePropertyChanged));
			EnableHighlightingProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("EnableHighlighting", typeof(bool), typeof(TreeMapControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, OnEnableHighlightingPropertyChanged));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<TreeMapControl>();
		}

		private static void DataAdapterPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ElementHelper.ChangeOwner(d, e);
			if (d is TreeMapControl treeMapControl)
			{
				treeMapControl.UpdateDataAdapter();
			}
		}

		private static void LayoutAlgorithmPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapControl treeMapControl)
			{
				treeMapControl.ActualLayoutAlgorithm = e.NewValue as TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase;
			}
		}

		private static void ColorizerPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapControl treeMapControl)
			{
				CommonUtils.SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(e.OldValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, e.NewValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, treeMapControl);
				treeMapControl.ColorizerChanged();
			}
		}

		private static void TemplateChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapControl { ActualItems: not null } treeMapControl)
			{
				treeMapControl.UpdateItemsTemplate(treeMapControl.ActualItems);
			}
		}

		private static void TreeMapStylePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapControl treeMapControl)
			{
				TreeMapStyle treeMapStyle = e.NewValue as TreeMapStyle;
				CommonUtils.SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(e.OldValue as INotifyPropertyChanged, treeMapStyle, treeMapControl);
				if (treeMapStyle != null)
				{
					treeMapControl.ApplyAppearance();
				}
			}
		}

		private static void OnEnableHighlightingPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is TreeMapControl treeMapControl)
			{
				treeMapControl.ViewController.SetActualItemHighlighting((bool)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		public TreeMapControl()
		{
			actualLayoutAlgorithm = new SquarifiedLayoutAlgorithm();
			base.Loaded += delegate
			{
				InvalidateLayout();
			};
		}

		void ITreeMapLayoutCalculator.CalculateLayout(IList<ITreeMapLayoutItem> treeMapItems, Size constraint, int groupLevel)
		{
			if (treeMapItems.Count > 0 && constraint.Height != 0.0 && constraint.Width != 0.0)
			{
				ActualLayoutAlgorithm.Calculate(treeMapItems, constraint, groupLevel);
				base.ViewController.HideToolTip();
			}
		}

		TreeMapItem INativeItemsCollector.ProcessAddNativeItem(object item, object parent)
		{
			if (nativeDataSource == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return nativeDataSource.ProcessAddNativeItem(item, parent);
		}

		void INativeItemsCollector.ProcessRemoveNativeItem(object item, object parent)
		{
			if (nativeDataSource != null)
			{
				nativeDataSource.ProcessRemoveNativeItem(item, parent);
			}
		}

		void INativeItemsCollector.Clear()
		{
			ClearCollector();
		}

		TreeMapItemPositionInfo INativeItemsCollector.GetItemPositionInfo(TreeMapItem item)
		{
			if (nativeDataSource == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return nativeDataSource.GetItemPositionInfo(item);
		}

		bool ITreeMapItemColorizer.TryColorizeTreeMapItem(TreeMapItem item)
		{
			if (nativeDataSource != null)
			{
				TreeMapItemPositionInfo itemPositionInfo = nativeDataSource.GetItemPositionInfo(item);
				if (itemPositionInfo != null)
				{
					ColorizerController.ColorizeItem(item, itemPositionInfo.GroupLevel, itemPositionInfo.GroupIndex, itemPositionInfo.ItemIndex);
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (e is PropertyChangedEventArgs)
			{
				if (sender is TreeMapStyle)
				{
					ApplyAppearance();
					return true;
				}
				if (sender is TreeMapColorizerBase)
				{
					ColorizeItemsAndFillLegend();
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void ClearCollector()
		{
			if (nativeDataSource != null)
			{
				nativeDataSource.Clear();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDataAdapter()
		{
			if (itemsContainer != null)
			{
				nativeDataSource = DataAdapter as ISupportNativeSource;
				if (nativeDataSource == null)
				{
					ItemsContainer.ItemsSource = ActualItems;
					ColorizeItemsAndFillLegend();
					UpdateSelection();
				}
				else
				{
					ItemsContainer.ItemsSource = nativeDataSource.GetNativeSource();
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateItemsTemplate(TreeMapItemCollection items)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in items)
			{
				item.UpdateTemplate();
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					UpdateItemsTemplate(item.Children);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ApplyAppearance()
		{
			base.ToolTipInfo.ToolTipBackground = TreeMapStyle.ActualToolTipBackground;
			base.ToolTipInfo.ToolTipForeground = TreeMapStyle.ActualToolTipForeground;
			base.ToolTipInfo.ToolTipFontSize = TreeMapStyle.ActualToolTipFontSize;
			base.ToolTipInfo.ToolTipFontWeight = TreeMapStyle.ActualToolTipFontWeight;
			ApplyItemsAppearance(ActualItems);
		}

		private void ApplyItemsAppearance(IEnumerable<TreeMapItem> items)
		{
			if (items == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in items)
			{
				item.ApplyAppearance();
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					ApplyItemsAppearance(item.Children);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void ColorizeItems()
		{
			if (Colorizer != null)
			{
				TreeMapItemCollection actualItems = ActualItems;
				if (actualItems != null && actualItems.Count > 0)
				{
					ColorizerController.ColorizeItems(actualItems);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override PatternDataProvider GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new TreeMapItemPatternProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		protected override ViewControllerBase CreateViewController()
		{
			return new TreeMapViewController(this);
		}

		protected override SelectedItemsControllerBase CreateSelectionController()
		{
			return new TreeMapSelectedItemsController(this);
		}

		internal void InvalidateLayout()
		{
			if (!isUpdatesLocked && Panel != null)
			{
				Panel.InvalidateMeasure();
				UpdateItems();
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateItems()
		{
			if (!isUpdatesLocked)
			{
				ColorizeItemsAndFillLegend();
				UpdateSelection();
			}
		}

		internal void InvalidateData()
		{
			if (nativeDataSource != null)
			{
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Loaded, (Action)delegate
				{
					InvalidateLayout();
				});
			}
			else
			{
				InvalidateLayout();
			}
		}

		protected internal override HierarchicalControlHitInfo CalcHitInfoCore(Point point)
		{
			return new TreeMapHitInfo(base.HitTestController, point);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			if (GetTemplateChild("PART_ItemsContainer") is TreeMapItemsControl treeMapItemsControl)
			{
				treeMapItemsControl.Interaction = new InteractionData(this);
				itemsContainer = new WeakReference(treeMapItemsControl);
			}
			ClearCollector();
			UpdateDataAdapter();
		}

		public TreeMapHitInfo CalcHitInfo(Point point)
		{
			return (TreeMapHitInfo)CalcHitInfoCore(point);
		}

		public void BeginDataUpdate()
		{
			isUpdatesLocked = true;
		}

		public void EndDataUpdate()
		{
			isUpdatesLocked = false;
			InvalidateData();
		}

		public List<IHierarchicalItem> GetItemPath(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			return TreeMapItemHelper.GetItemPath(item, ActualItems);
		}
	}

		event PropertyChangedEventHandler INotifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				propertyChanged = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(propertyChanged, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				propertyChanged = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(propertyChanged, value);
			}
		}

		private static DependencyProperty RegisterDP<T>(string propertyName, Action<TreeMapStyle, T> setActualProperty)
		{
			return DependencyProperty.Register(propertyName, typeof(T), typeof(TreeMapStyle), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				AppearanceChanged(d, e, setActualProperty);
			}));
		}

		private static void AppearanceChanged<T>(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e, Action<TreeMapStyle, T> setActualProperty)
		{
			TreeMapStyle style = d as TreeMapStyle;
			if (style != null)
			{
				style.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					setActualProperty(style, (T)e.NewValue);
					style.RaisePropertyChanged(e.Property.Name);
				});
			}
		}

		PatternDataProvider IPatternHolder.GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new TreeMapItemPatternProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		public TreeMapStyle()
		{
			BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(40, 0, 0, 0));
			BorderBrush.Freeze();
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			propertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}
	}

namespace DevExpress.Xpf.TreeMap.Native
{
	public delegate void BindingHandler();
	public class BindingBehavior
	{
		private readonly IFlatDataAdapter dataAdapter;

		private DataSet dataSet;

		private DataSourceProvider dataSourceProvider;

		private object dataSource;

		private object actualDataSource;

		public object ActualDataSource => actualDataSource;

		public event BindingHandler ActualDataSourceChanged;

		public BindingBehavior(IFlatDataAdapter dataAdapter)
		{
			this.dataAdapter = dataAdapter;
		}

		private void CreateDataSet(XmlNode node)
		{
			XmlNodeReader xmlNodeReader = new XmlNodeReader(node);
			try
			{
				dataSet = new DataSet();
				dataSet.ReadXml(xmlNodeReader);
			}
			finally
			{
				xmlNodeReader.Close();
			}
		}

		private void DataProviderDataChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (dataSourceProvider != null)
			{
				SetDataSource(dataSourceProvider.Data);
			}
			if (this.ActualDataSourceChanged != null)
			{
				this.ActualDataSourceChanged();
			}
		}

		private void ClearDataSet()
		{
			if (dataSet != null)
			{
				dataSet.Dispose();
				dataSet = null;
			}
		}

		private object PerformListDataSource(object dataSource)
		{
			if (dataSource is ICollection { Count: 1 } collection)
			{
				IEnumerator enumerator = collection.GetEnumerator();
				if (enumerator != null && enumerator.MoveNext() && enumerator.Current is XmlNode node)
				{
					CreateDataSet(node);
					return dataSet;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dataAdapter.DataMember))
			{
				return dataSource;
			}
			return DataBindingHelper.ExtractDataSource(dataSource, true, false, true);
		}

		private object PerformXmlNode(XmlNode node)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			while (node.ParentNode != null && !(node.ParentNode is XmlDocument))
			{
				text = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) ? (node.Name + "." + text) : node.Name);
				node = node.ParentNode;
			}
			CreateDataSet(node);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return dataSet.Tables[text];
			}
			return dataSet.Tables[0];
		}

		private void BeforeUpdateDataSource()
		{
			if (dataSourceProvider != null)
			{
				dataSourceProvider.DataChanged -= DataProviderDataChanged;
			}
		}

		private object PerformNewDataSource(object newDataSource)
		{
			dataSourceProvider = newDataSource as DataSourceProvider;
			if (dataSourceProvider != null)
			{
				dataSourceProvider.DataChanged += DataProviderDataChanged;
				return dataSourceProvider.Data;
			}
			if (newDataSource is MarkupExtension markupExtension)
			{
				return markupExtension.ProvideValue(null);
			}
			return newDataSource;
		}

		private void ProcessActualDataSource()
		{
			ClearDataSet();
			if (dataSource is IList || dataSource is IListSource)
			{
				actualDataSource = PerformListDataSource(dataSource);
			}
			else if (!(dataSource is IEnumerable enumerable))
			{
				actualDataSource = null;
			}
			else if (!(enumerable is XmlNode node))
			{
				ICollectionView collectionView = enumerable as ICollectionView;
				actualDataSource = ((collectionView != null) ? collectionView.SourceCollection : enumerable);
			}
			else
			{
				actualDataSource = PerformXmlNode(node);
			}
		}

		private void ClearDataSource()
		{
			actualDataSource = null;
			dataSource = null;
			ClearDataSet();
		}

		private void SetDataSource(object dataSource)
		{
			ClearDataSource();
			if (dataSource != null)
			{
				this.dataSource = dataSource;
				ProcessActualDataSource();
			}
		}

		public void UpdateActualDataSource(object newDataSource)
		{
			BeforeUpdateDataSource();
			if (newDataSource != null)
			{
				SetDataSource(PerformNewDataSource(newDataSource));
			}
			else
			{
				ClearDataSource();
			}
			this.ActualDataSourceChanged();
		}
	}
	public class RangeColorizerBehavior : LegendSupportColorizerBehavior
	{
		private const string defaultLegendPattern = "{V1} - {V2}";

		private readonly List<double> sortedRangeStops = new List<double>();

		private DoubleCollection rangeStops;

		private bool shouldSortRangeStops = true;

		private List<double> SortedRangeStops
		{
			get
			{
				CheckSortedRangeStops();
				return sortedRangeStops;
			}
		}

		protected override string DefaultLegendPattern => "{V1} - {V2}";

		public RangeColorizerBehavior()
			: base(WpfLegendItemCreator.Instance)
		{
		}

		private void RangeStops_Changed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			shouldSortRangeStops = true;
		}

		private void CheckSortedRangeStops()
		{
			if (shouldSortRangeStops)
			{
				sortedRangeStops.Clear();
				sortedRangeStops.AddRange(rangeStops);
				sortedRangeStops.Remove(double.NaN);
				sortedRangeStops.Remove(double.MaxValue);
				sortedRangeStops.Remove(double.MinValue);
				sortedRangeStops.Sort(Comparer<double>.Default);
			}
		}

		private Color CalculateApproximatedColor(double normalized, PaletteBase colors)
		{
			double num = normalized * (double)(colors.Count - 1);
			int num2 = (int)Math.Floor(num);
			double ratio = num - (double)num2;
			return ColorizerCalculator.MixColors(colors[num2], colors[num2 + 1], ratio);
		}

		private int GetRangeIndex(double value)
		{
			List<double> list = SortedRangeStops;
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count - 1; i++)
			{
				if (value >= list[i] && value < list[i + 1])
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		public void UpdateRangeStops(DoubleCollection rangeStops)
		{
			this.rangeStops = rangeStops;
			shouldSortRangeStops = true;
		}

		protected override PatternDataProvider CreateDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new ValueRangeLegendItemDataProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		public Color? GetItemColor(double value, PaletteBase colors)
		{
			if (colors != null && colors.Count > 0 && rangeStops != null && rangeStops.Count > 0)
			{
				int rangeIndex = GetRangeIndex(value);
				if (rangeIndex >= 0)
				{
					if (colors.Count < rangeStops.Count)
					{
						double normalized = (double)rangeIndex / (double)(rangeStops.Count - 1);
						return CalculateApproximatedColor(normalized, colors);
					}
					return colors[rangeIndex];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(PaletteBase palette)
		{
			List<ILegendItem> list = new List<ILegendItem>();
			List<double> list2 = SortedRangeStops;
			int num = list2.Count - 1;
			if (palette.Count == 0 || num == 0)
			{
				return list;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				double num2 = list2[i];
				double num3 = list2[i + 1];
				string text = GetText(new double[2] { num2, num3 });
				Color color = ((palette.Count < num) ? CalculateApproximatedColor((double)i / (double)(num - 1), palette) : palette[i]);
				list.Add(new LegendItem
				{
					Text = text,
					Brush = new SolidColorBrush(color)
				});
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class ColorCollectionTypeConverter : ExpandableObjectConverter
	{
		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destType)
		{
			if (!(destType == typeof(string)))
			{
				return destType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			return sourceType == typeof(string);
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destType)
		{
			if (value is ColorCollection)
			{
				if (destType == typeof(string))
				{
					return ((IFormattable)value).ToString(null, culture);
				}
				if (destType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor))
				{
					ConstructorInfo constructor = value.GetType().GetConstructor(new Type[1] { typeof(string) });
					if (constructor != null)
					{
						return new InstanceDescriptor(constructor, new object[1] { ((ColorCollection)value).ToString() });
					}
				}
			}
			return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destType);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			ColorCollection colorCollection = new ColorCollection();
			if (value is string text)
			{
				string[] array = text.Split(' ');
				if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
				{
					string[] array2 = array;
					foreach (string value2 in array2)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value2))
						{
							try
							{
								Color item = (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(value2);
								colorCollection.Add(item);
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return colorCollection;
		}
	}
	public class PaletteTypeConverter : ExpandableObjectConverter
	{
		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(ITypeDescriptorContext context, object value, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = base.GetProperties(context, value, attributes);
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = properties.Find("ScaleMode", ignoreCase: false);
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection(new PropertyDescriptor[1] { propertyDescriptor });
		}
	}
	public static class ColorizerCalculator
	{
		private static byte MixChannel(byte fromValue, byte toValue, double ratio)
		{
			return (byte)((double)(int)fromValue * (1.0 - ratio) + (double)(int)toValue * ratio);
		}

		public static Color MixColors(Color fromUnit, Color toUnit, double ratio)
		{
			return Color.FromArgb(MixChannel(fromUnit.A, toUnit.A, ratio), MixChannel(fromUnit.R, toUnit.R, ratio), MixChannel(fromUnit.G, toUnit.G, ratio), MixChannel(fromUnit.B, toUnit.B, ratio));
		}
	}
	public class ColorizerController
	{
		private struct MinMax
		{
			private double min;

			private double max;

			public static MinMax Empty => new MinMax(double.NaN, double.NaN);

			public double Min => min;

			public double Max => max;

			public MinMax(double min, double max)
			{
				this.min = min;
				this.max = max;
			}

			public void ApplyValue(double value)
			{
				if (!double.IsNaN(value))
				{
					min = (double.IsNaN(min) ? value : Math.Min(value, min));
					max = (double.IsNaN(max) ? value : Math.Max(value, max));
				}
			}
		}

		private readonly ColorizerBrushCache colorizerBrushCache;

		private TreeMapColorizerBase Colorizer => TreeMap.Colorizer;

		protected TreeMapControl TreeMap { get; }

		public ColorizerController(TreeMapControl treeMap)
		{
			TreeMap = treeMap;
			colorizerBrushCache = new ColorizerBrushCache();
		}

		private MinMax CalculateMinMax(TreeMapItemCollection items)
		{
			MinMax empty = MinMax.Empty;
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in items)
			{
				empty.ApplyValue(item.GetActualValue());
			}
			return empty;
		}

		protected SolidColorBrush GetItemBrush(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			Color? itemColor = Colorizer.GetItemColor(item, group);
			if (itemColor.HasValue)
			{
				return colorizerBrushCache.GetBrush(itemColor.Value);
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual int ColorizeItems(TreeMapItemCollection items, int groupLevel, int groupIndex)
		{
			int num = 1;
			MinMax minMax = CalculateMinMax(items);
			for (int i = 0; i < items.Count; i++)
			{
				TreeMapItem treeMapItem = items[i];
				treeMapItem.ColorizerBrush = GetItemBrush(treeMapItem, new TreeMapItemGroupInfo(groupLevel, groupIndex, i, minMax.Min, minMax.Max));
				if (treeMapItem.IsGroup)
				{
					num += ColorizeItems(treeMapItem.Children, groupLevel + 1, groupIndex + num);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void ColorizeItem(TreeMapItem item, int groupLevel, int groupIndex, int itemIndex)
		{
			if (Colorizer != null)
			{
				MinMax minMax = CalculateMinMax(item.Children);
				item.ColorizerBrush = GetItemBrush(item, new TreeMapItemGroupInfo(groupLevel, groupIndex, itemIndex, minMax.Min, minMax.Max));
			}
		}

		public void ColorizeItems(TreeMapItemCollection items)
		{
			ColorizeItems(items, 0, 0);
		}

		public void ClearBrushCache()
		{
			colorizerBrushCache.Clear();
		}
	}
	public class SpreadsheetColorizerController : ColorizerController
	{
		public SpreadsheetColorizerController(TreeMapControl treeMap)
			: base(treeMap)
		{
		}

		protected override int ColorizeItems(TreeMapItemCollection items, int groupLevel, int groupIndex)
		{
			if (items[0].IsGroup)
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (TreeMapItem item in items.OrderBy((TreeMapItem i) => base.TreeMap.GroupIndexProvider.GetGroupIndex(((IHierarchicalItem)i).Label)))
				{
					ColorizeItems(item.Children, groupLevel + 1, num++);
				}
			}
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < items.Count; num2++)
			{
				items[num2].ColorizerBrush = GetItemBrush(items[num2], new TreeMapItemGroupInfo(groupLevel, groupIndex, num2, 0.0, 0.0));
			}
			return 0;
		}
	}
	public class ColorizerBrushCache
	{
		private readonly Dictionary<Color, SolidColorBrush> brushCache = new Dictionary<Color, SolidColorBrush>();

		public SolidColorBrush GetBrush(Color color)
		{
			if (!brushCache.TryGetValue(color, out var value))
			{
				value = new SolidColorBrush(color);
				brushCache.Add(color, value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			brushCache.Clear();
		}
	}
	public interface INativeItemsCollector

	public class TreeMapItemPositionInfo
	{
		public int GroupLevel { get; private set; }

		public int GroupIndex { get; private set; }

		public int ItemIndex { get; private set; }

		public TreeMapItemPositionInfo(int groupIndex, int groupLevel, int itemIndex)
		{
			GroupIndex = groupIndex;
			GroupLevel = groupLevel;
			ItemIndex = itemIndex;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemsCollector
	{
		private class TreeMapNode
		{
			private List<TreeMapNode> children = new List<TreeMapNode>();

			public TreeMapItem Item { get; private set; }

			public TreeMapItemPositionInfo PositionInfo { get; private set; }

			public List<TreeMapNode> Children => children;

			public int ParentGroupIndex { get; set; }

			public TreeMapNode(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemPositionInfo positionInfo)
			{
				Item = item;
				PositionInfo = positionInfo;
			}
		}

		private List<TreeMapNode> root = new List<TreeMapNode>();

		private int groupIndex;

		private readonly TreeMapItemCollection treeMapItems;

		private Dictionary<object, TreeMapNode> mapping = new Dictionary<object, TreeMapNode>();

		public TreeMapItemsCollector(TreeMapItemCollection treeMapItems)
		{
			this.treeMapItems = treeMapItems;
		}

		internal void UpdateItemValue(object nativeItem, double value)
		{
			mapping[nativeItem].Item.Value = value;
		}

		internal TreeMapItemPositionInfo FindPlace(TreeMapItem item)
		{
			return FindNode(item, root)?.PositionInfo;
		}

		private TreeMapNode FindNode(TreeMapItem item, List<TreeMapNode> nodes)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapNode node in nodes)
			{
				if (node.Item == item)
				{
					return node;
				}
				TreeMapNode treeMapNode = FindNode(item, node.Children);
				if (treeMapNode != null)
				{
					return treeMapNode;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public TreeMapItem ProcessAddNativeItem(object item, object parent, double value)
		{
			TreeMapItem treeMapItem = null;
			if (!mapping.ContainsKey(item))
			{
				treeMapItem = new TreeMapItem
				{
					Tag = item,
					Value = value
				};
				TreeMapNode value2;
				if (parent == null)
				{
					TreeMapNode treeMapNode = new TreeMapNode(treeMapItem, new TreeMapItemPositionInfo(0, 0, root.Count));
					mapping.Add(item, treeMapNode);
					treeMapItems.Add(treeMapItem);
					root.Add(treeMapNode);
				}
				else if (mapping.TryGetValue(parent, out value2))
				{
					if (value2.ParentGroupIndex == 0)
					{
						value2.ParentGroupIndex = ++groupIndex;
					}
					TreeMapNode treeMapNode2 = new TreeMapNode(treeMapItem, new TreeMapItemPositionInfo(value2.ParentGroupIndex, value2.PositionInfo.GroupLevel + 1, value2.Children.Count));
					value2.Children.Add(treeMapNode2);
					value2.Item.Children.Add(treeMapItem);
					mapping.Add(item, treeMapNode2);
				}
			}
			return treeMapItem;
		}

		public void ProcessRemoveNativeItem(object item, object parent)
		{
			if (!mapping.ContainsKey(parent) || !mapping.ContainsKey(item))
			{
				return;
			}
			TreeMapNode treeMapNode = mapping[item];
			foreach (TreeMapNode child in treeMapNode.Children)
			{
				ProcessRemoveNativeItem(child, treeMapNode);
			}
			mapping.Remove(item);
			TreeMapNode treeMapNode2 = mapping[parent];
			treeMapNode2.Children.Remove(treeMapNode);
			treeMapNode2.Item.Children.Remove(treeMapNode.Item);
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			mapping.Clear();
			treeMapItems.Clear();
			root.Clear();
			groupIndex = 0;
		}
	}
	public class HierarchicalDataController : IWeakEventListener
	{
		private TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter adapter;

		public HierarchicalDataController(TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter adapter)
		{
			this.adapter = adapter;
		}

		private bool ProcessWeakEvent(object sender)
		{
			Type type = sender.GetType();
			PropertyInfo property = type.GetProperty(adapter.ValueDataMember);
			if (property != null)
			{
				adapter.UpdateItemValue(sender, Convert.ToDouble(property.GetValue(sender, null)));
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal void SubscribePropertiesChanged(object item)
		{
			if (item != null)
			{
				CommonUtils.SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(item as INotifyPropertyChanged, item as INotifyPropertyChanged, this);
			}
		}

		public bool ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (e is PropertyChangedEventArgs e2 && e2.PropertyName == adapter.ValueDataMember)
			{
				if (managerType == typeof(PropertyChangedWeakEventManager))
				{
					return ProcessWeakEvent(sender);
				}
				return false;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class HierarchicalControlGroupController : GroupController
	{
		private readonly BindingBehavior bindingBehavior;

		public override object DataSource
		{
			get
			{
				return bindingBehavior.ActualDataSource;
			}
			set
			{
				bindingBehavior.UpdateActualDataSource(value);
			}
		}

		public HierarchicalControlGroupController(IFlatDataAdapter dataAdapter)
			: base(dataAdapter)
		{
			bindingBehavior = new BindingBehavior(dataAdapter);
			bindingBehavior.ActualDataSourceChanged += delegate
			{
				UpdateData();
			};
		}

		protected override void OnItemChanged(ListChangedEventArgs e, object row, int rowIndex)
		{
			ChunkListChangedEventArgs e2 = (ChunkListChangedEventArgs)(object)((e is ChunkListChangedEventArgs) ? e : null);
			if (e2 == null || ((ListChangedEventArgs)(object)e2).PropertyDescriptor != null || e2.OldItem == null)
			{
				base.OnItemChanged(e, row, rowIndex);
			}
			else
			{
				ReplaceItem(row, rowIndex, e2.OldItem);
			}
		}

		protected override Predicate<IHierarchicalItem> GetDeleteItemPredicate(ListChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			return delegate(IHierarchicalItem item)
			{
				if (!base.GetDeleteItemPredicate(e)(item))
				{
					object tag = item.Tag;
					ListChangedEventArgs e2 = e;
					ListChangedEventArgs obj = ((e2 is ChunkListChangedEventArgs) ? e2 : null);
					return tag.Equals((obj != null) ? ((ChunkListChangedEventArgs)obj).OldItem : null);
				}
				return true;
			};
		}
	}
	public class HitTestController
	{
		private Visual hierarchicalControl;

		private List<IHitTestableElement> currentElements;

		public HitTestController(Visual hierarchicalControl)
		{
			this.hierarchicalControl = hierarchicalControl;
		}

		private void AddUniqueHitTestableElement(IHitTestableElement hitTestableElement)
		{
			if (hitTestableElement != null && !currentElements.Contains(hitTestableElement))
			{
				currentElements.Add(hitTestableElement);
			}
		}

		private IHitTestableElement GetParentHitTestableElement(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = obj;
			while (dependencyObject != null && !(dependencyObject is TreeMapControl))
			{
				if (dependencyObject is IHitTestableElement result)
				{
					return result;
				}
				dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private HitTestResultBehavior OnHitTestResult(HitTestResult result)
		{
			AddUniqueHitTestableElement(GetParentHitTestableElement(result.VisualHit));
			return HitTestResultBehavior.Continue;
		}

		private void PrepareHitTestableElements(Point point)
		{
			VisualTreeHelper.HitTest(hierarchicalControl, null, OnHitTestResult, new PointHitTestParameters(point));
		}

		private void PrepareHitTestableElements(Rect region)
		{
			Geometry geometry = new RectangleGeometry(region);
			VisualTreeHelper.HitTest(hierarchicalControl, null, OnHitTestResult, new GeometryHitTestParameters(geometry));
		}

		public T FindElements<T>(Point point) where T : class, IHitTestableElement
		{
			currentElements = new List<IHitTestableElement>();
			PrepareHitTestableElements(point);
			return currentElements.Find((IHitTestableElement element) => element is T && element.IsHitTestVisible) as T;
		}
	}
	public interface IOwnedElement

	}
	public interface IHitTestableElement
	{
		object Element { get; }

		bool IsHitTestVisible { get; }
	}
