		}

			return new SunburstController();

	public class SunburstController : HierarchicalChartController
	{
		protected override HierarchicalChartControlBase CreateHierarchicalChartControl()
		{
			return new SunburstControl();
		}

		protected override HierarchicalChartConfigurator CreateConfigurator(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase)
		{
			return new SunburstConfigurator((SunburstControl)hierarchicalChartControlBase);
		}

	public class SunburstConfigurator : HierarchicalChartConfigurator
	{
		public SunburstControl Sunburst => (SunburstControl)base.HierarchicalControl;

		public SunburstConfigurator(SunburstControl sunburst)
			: base(sunburst)
		{
		}

		private SunburstDataAdapterBase CreateFlatAdapter(HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter modelFlatDataAdapter)
		{
			SunburstFlatDataAdapter sunburstFlatDataAdapter = new SunburstFlatDataAdapter
			{
				LabelDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.LabelDataMember,
				ValueDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ValueDataMember,
				ColorDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ColorDataMember
			};
			if (modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers != null)
			{
				string[] groupDataMembers = modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers;
				foreach (string item in groupDataMembers)
				{
					sunburstFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers.Add(item);
				}
			}
			return sunburstFlatDataAdapter;
		}

		private SunburstDataAdapterBase CreateHierarchicalAdapter(HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter modelHierarchicalDataAdapter)
		{
			SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter = new SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter();
			foreach (HierarchicalChartDataMapping mapping in modelHierarchicalDataAdapter.Mappings)
			{
				sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter.Mappings.Add(new SunburstDataMapping
				{
					LabelDataMember = mapping.LabelDataMember,
					ValueDataMember = mapping.ValueDataMember,
					ColorDataMember = mapping.ColorDataMember,
					ChildrenDataMember = mapping.ChildrenDataMember,
					Type = mapping.Type
				});
			}
			return sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter;
		}

		private void UpdateItemsAppearance(HierarchicalChart model, Sunburst sunburstModel)
		{
			HierarchicalItemAppearance itemAppearance = sunburstModel.ItemAppearance;
			if (itemAppearance == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Brush stroke = (itemAppearance.BorderVisible ? CreateSolidBrush(itemAppearance.BorderColor) : Brushes.Transparent);
			float borderBrightnessCoeff = model.ItemAppearance.BorderBrightnessCoeff;
			LabelAppearance labelAppearance = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance;
			Brush foreground = ((labelAppearance != null) ? CreateSolidBrush(labelAppearance.TextColor) : null);
			Color color = labelAppearance?.TextGlowColor.ToColor() ?? Colors.Black;
			FontModel fontModel = labelAppearance?.Font;
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo in Sunburst.SectorViewInfos)
			{
				if (sectorViewInfo.Fill is SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush && borderBrightnessCoeff != 1f)
				{
					Color color2 = solidColorBrush.Color;
					sectorViewInfo.Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(color2.A, (byte)Math.Min(255f, (float)(int)color2.R * borderBrightnessCoeff), (byte)Math.Min(255f, (float)(int)color2.G * borderBrightnessCoeff), (byte)Math.Min(255f, (float)(int)color2.B * borderBrightnessCoeff)));
				}
				else
				{
					sectorViewInfo.Stroke = stroke;
				}
				sectorViewInfo.Foreground = foreground;
				sectorViewInfo.LabelVisible = labelAppearance.Visible;
				sectorViewInfo.Shadow = null;
				if (fontModel != null && sectorViewInfo.LabelVisible)
				{
					ConfigureFont(sectorViewInfo, fontModel);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void SetDataAdapter(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			SunburstDataAdapterBase sunburstDataAdapterBase = ((model.DataAdapter is HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter modelFlatDataAdapter) ? CreateFlatAdapter(modelFlatDataAdapter) : CreateHierarchicalAdapter((HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter)model.DataAdapter));
			sunburstDataAdapterBase.UpdateIsSpreadSheetChart(isSpreadSheetChart: true);
			Sunburst.DataAdapter = sunburstDataAdapterBase;
			sunburstDataAdapterBase.DataSource = model.DataSource;
		}

		protected override void SetColorizer(HierarchicalChart treeMapModel, CustomPalette palette, bool hasGroupData)
		{
			Sunburst.Colorizer = new SunburstColorObjectColorizer
			{
				Palette = palette,
				VaryColorInGroup = !hasGroupData
			};
		}

		protected override void ConfigureCore(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			Sunburst.DataAdapter.LabelFormatter = new ModelSupportLabelFormatter(model.LabelFormatter);
			Sunburst.HighlightMode = SunburstHighlightMode.None;
			Sunburst sunburstModel = (Sunburst)model;
			Sunburst.StartAngle = sunburstModel.StartAngle;
			Sunburst.HoleRadiusPercent = sunburstModel.HoleRadiusPercent;
			Sunburst.Loaded += delegate
			{
				UpdateItemsAppearance(model, sunburstModel);
			};
			Sunburst.DataAdapter.ItemsChanged += delegate
			{
				UpdateItemsAppearance(model, sunburstModel);
			};
		}
	}
	public class SunburstColorObjectColorizer : SunburstPaletteColorizer
	{
		protected override Color GetItemColor(ISunburstSectorInfo item)
		{
			object obj = (item as SunburstSectorViewInfo)?.DataSourceColor;
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return base.GetItemColor(item);
			}
			Color? color = ColorTranslator.ConvertToColor(obj);
			if (!color.HasValue)
			{
				return Colors.Transparent;
			}
			return color.Value;
		}
	}
	public class HierarchicalChartRenderContext : IChartRenderContext
	{
		public ModelRect Bounds { get; }

		public ContentPresenter Container { get; }

		public HierarchicalChartRenderContext(ContentPresenter container, ModelRect bounds)
		{
			Container = container;
			Bounds = bounds;
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalChartModelControllerFactory : ModelControllerFactoryBase
	{
		public override IChartRenderContext CreateRenderContext(ModelRect bounds, params object[] renderParams)
		{
			if (renderParams.Length != 0 && renderParams[0] is ContentPresenter container)
			{
				return new HierarchicalChartRenderContext(container, bounds);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalChartController : Controller
	{
		private readonly HierarchicalChartConfigurator configurator;

		private readonly HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControl;

		protected HierarchicalChartController()
		{
			hierarchicalChartControl = CreateHierarchicalChartControl();
			configurator = CreateConfigurator(hierarchicalChartControl);
		}

		protected abstract HierarchicalChartControlBase CreateHierarchicalChartControl();

		protected abstract HierarchicalChartConfigurator CreateConfigurator(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase);

		public override HitInfo CalcHitInfo(double x, double y)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public override void ClearSelection()
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public override void Deselect(ModelElement element)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public override void Select(ModelElement element)
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		protected override void OnModelChanged()
		{
			configurator.Configure((HierarchicalChart)base.ChartModel);
		}

		public override void RenderChart(IChartRenderContext renderContext)
		{
			if (renderContext is HierarchicalChartRenderContext { Container: not null } hierarchicalChartRenderContext)
			{
				hierarchicalChartControl.Width = hierarchicalChartRenderContext.Bounds.Width;
				hierarchicalChartControl.Height = hierarchicalChartRenderContext.Bounds.Height;
				hierarchicalChartRenderContext.Container.Content = hierarchicalChartControl;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapModelControllerFactory : HierarchicalChartModelControllerFactory
	{
		public override IController CreateController()
		{
			return new TreeMapController();
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapController : HierarchicalChartController
	{
		protected override HierarchicalChartControlBase CreateHierarchicalChartControl()
		{
			return new TreeMapControl();
		}

		protected override HierarchicalChartConfigurator CreateConfigurator(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalChartControlBase)
		{
			return new TreeMapConfigurator((TreeMapControl)hierarchicalChartControlBase);
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalChartConfigurator
	{
		protected HierarchicalChartControlBase HierarchicalControl { get; }

		protected HierarchicalChartConfigurator(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
		{
			HierarchicalControl = hierarchicalControl;
		}

		private Brush CreateFillStyleBrush(FillStyle fillStyle, ColorARGB color)
		{
			if (fillStyle == null || fillStyle.FillMode != FillMode.Gradient || fillStyle.Options == null)
			{
				return CreateSolidBrush(color);
			}
			return CreateGradientBrush(fillStyle, color);
		}

		private LinearGradientBrush CreateGradientBrush(FillStyle fillStyle, ColorARGB color)
		{
			LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush = new LinearGradientBrush();
			GradientStop value = new GradientStop
			{
				Color = color.ToColor(),
				Offset = 0.0
			};
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(value);
			GradientStop value2 = new GradientStop
			{
				Color = fillStyle.Options.Color2.ToColor(),
				Offset = 1.0
			};
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(value2);
			GradientStop gradientStop = null;
			switch (fillStyle.Options.GradientMode)
			{
			case GradientMode.BottomLeftToTopRight:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 1.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(1.0, 0.0);
				break;
			case GradientMode.BottomRightToTopLeft:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(1.0, 1.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				break;
			case GradientMode.BottomToTop:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.5, 1.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.5, 0.0);
				break;
			case GradientMode.FromCenterHorizontal:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(1.0, 0.0);
				gradientStop = new GradientStop
				{
					Color = fillStyle.Options.Color2.ToColor(),
					Offset = 0.0
				};
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Insert(0, gradientStop);
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[1].Offset = 0.5;
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[2].Offset = 1.0;
				break;
			case GradientMode.FromCenterVertical:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.0, 1.0);
				gradientStop = new GradientStop
				{
					Color = fillStyle.Options.Color2.ToColor(),
					Offset = 0.0
				};
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Insert(0, gradientStop);
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[1].Offset = 0.5;
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[2].Offset = 1.0;
				break;
			case GradientMode.ToCenterHorizontal:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(1.0, 0.0);
				gradientStop = new GradientStop
				{
					Color = color.ToColor(),
					Offset = 1.0
				};
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(gradientStop);
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[1].Offset = 0.5;
				break;
			case GradientMode.ToCenterVertical:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.0, 1.0);
				gradientStop = new GradientStop
				{
					Color = color.ToColor(),
					Offset = 1.0
				};
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(gradientStop);
				linearGradientBrush.GradientStops[1].Offset = 0.5;
				break;
			case GradientMode.LeftToRight:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.5);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(1.0, 0.5);
				break;
			case GradientMode.RightToLeft:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(1.0, 0.5);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.5);
				break;
			case GradientMode.TopLeftToBottomRight:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.0, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(1.0, 1.0);
				break;
			case GradientMode.TopRightToBottomLeft:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(1.0, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.0, 1.0);
				break;
			case GradientMode.TopToBottom:
				linearGradientBrush.StartPoint = new Point(0.5, 0.0);
				linearGradientBrush.EndPoint = new Point(0.5, 1.0);
				break;
			}
			return linearGradientBrush;
		}

		private void ConfigureShadow(UIElement element, Shadow shadowModel)
		{
			DropShadowEffect dropShadowEffect = new DropShadowEffect();
			if (!shadowModel.Color.IsEmpty)
			{
				dropShadowEffect.Color = shadowModel.Color.ToColor();
			}
			dropShadowEffect.ShadowDepth = shadowModel.Size;
			element.Effect = dropShadowEffect;
		}

		private void ConfigureBorder(Control control, DevExpress.Charts.Model.Border borderModel)
		{
			if (!borderModel.Color.IsEmpty)
			{
				control.BorderBrush = CreateSolidBrush(borderModel.Color);
			}
			control.BorderThickness = new Thickness(borderModel.Thickness);
		}

		private void ConfigurePadding(Control control, RectangleIndents indentsModel)
		{
			if (indentsModel.All != -1)
			{
				control.Padding = new Thickness(indentsModel.All);
			}
			else
			{
				control.Padding = new Thickness(indentsModel.Left, indentsModel.Top, indentsModel.Right, indentsModel.Bottom);
			}
		}

		private void ConfigureMargins(Control control, RectangleIndents indentsModel)
		{
			if (indentsModel.All != -1)
			{
				control.Margin = new Thickness(indentsModel.All);
			}
			else
			{
				control.Margin = new Thickness(indentsModel.Left, indentsModel.Top, indentsModel.Right, indentsModel.Bottom);
			}
		}

		private void ConfigColorizer(HierarchicalChart hierarchicalChartModel)
		{
			CustomPalette customPalette = new CustomPalette();
			foreach (PaletteEntry entry in hierarchicalChartModel.Palette.Entries)
			{
				customPalette.Colors.Add(entry.Color.ToColor());
			}
			SetColorizer(hierarchicalChartModel, customPalette, hierarchicalChartModel.DataAdapter.HasGroupData);
		}

		private void ConfigureChartTitles(HierarchicalChart model, ChartTitleAppearance appearance)
		{
			TitleCollection titleCollection = new TitleCollection();
			foreach (ChartTitle item in (DXCollectionBase<ChartTitle>)(object)model.Titles)
			{
				Title title = new Title();
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (item.Lines != null)
				{
					string[] lines = item.Lines;
					foreach (string text2 in lines)
					{
						text += text2;
						if (text2 != item.Lines[item.Lines.Length - 1])
						{
							text += "\n";
						}
					}
				}
				title.Content = text;
				if (item.Font != null)
				{
					ConfigureFont(title, item.Font);
				}
				if (appearance != null)
				{
					title.Foreground = CreateSolidBrush(appearance.TextColor);
				}
				title.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
				titleCollection.Add(title);
			}
			HierarchicalControl.Titles = titleCollection;
		}

		private void ConfigureLegend(Legend legend, DevExpress.Charts.Model.Legend legendModel, LegendAppearance appearance)
		{
			HorizontalPosition horizontalPosition = (legend.HorizontalPosition = (HorizontalPosition)LegendPositionConverter.ConverterToHorizontalAlignment(legendModel.LegendPosition, legendModel.Overlay));
			legend.VerticalPosition = (VerticalPosition)LegendPositionConverter.ConverterToVerticalAlignment(legendModel.LegendPosition, legendModel.Overlay);
			legend.Direction = ((horizontalPosition == HorizontalPosition.Center) ? LegendDirection.LeftToRight : LegendDirection.TopToBottom);
			if (appearance != null)
			{
				if (!appearance.BackColor.IsEmpty)
				{
					legend.Background = CreateFillStyleBrush(appearance.FillStyle, appearance.BackColor);
				}
				if (!appearance.TextColor.IsEmpty)
				{
					legend.Foreground = CreateSolidBrush(appearance.TextColor);
				}
				if (appearance.Border != null)
				{
					ConfigureBorder(legend, appearance.Border);
				}
				if (appearance.Shadow != null)
				{
					ConfigureShadow(legend, appearance.Shadow);
				}
				if (appearance.Margins != null)
				{
					ConfigureMargins(legend, appearance.Margins);
				}
				if (appearance.Padding != null)
				{
					ConfigurePadding(legend, appearance.Padding);
				}
			}
			FontModel font = legendModel.Font;
			if (font != null)
			{
				if (font.Bold)
				{
					legend.FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
				}
				if (font.Italic)
				{
					legend.FontStyle = FontStyles.Italic;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(font.Name))
				{
					legend.FontFamily = new FontFamily(font.Name);
				}
				legend.FontSize = FontConversion.ConvertFromPointToPixel(font.Size);
			}
		}

		protected abstract void SetDataAdapter(HierarchicalChart model);

		protected abstract void SetColorizer(HierarchicalChart treeMapModel, CustomPalette palette, bool hasGroupData);

		protected abstract void ConfigureCore(HierarchicalChart model);

		protected SolidColorBrush CreateSolidBrush(ColorARGB model)
		{
			SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush = new SolidColorBrush(model.ToColor());
			solidColorBrush.Freeze();
			return solidColorBrush;
		}

		protected void ConfigureFont(ISupportFontSettings supportFontElement, FontModel fontModel)
		{
			if (fontModel.Bold)
			{
				supportFontElement.FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
			}
			if (fontModel.Italic)
			{
				supportFontElement.FontStyle = FontStyles.Italic;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fontModel.Name))
			{
				supportFontElement.FontFamily = new FontFamily(fontModel.Name);
			}
			if (fontModel.Size != 0f)
			{
				supportFontElement.FontSize = FontConversion.ConvertFromPointToPixel(fontModel.Size);
			}
		}

		public void Configure(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			if (model == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			HierarchicalControl.IsSpreadsheetControl = true;
			ChartAppearance plotAppearance = model.PlotAppearance;
			if (plotAppearance != null)
			{
				HierarchicalControl.Background = CreateSolidBrush(plotAppearance.BackColor);
				if (model.PlotAppearance.Border != null)
				{
					ConfigureBorder(HierarchicalControl, plotAppearance.Border);
				}
			}
			if (model.Palette != null)
			{
				ConfigColorizer(model);
			}
			if (model.LabelFormatter != null)
			{
				HierarchicalControl.LabelFormatter = new ModelSupportLabelFormatter(model.LabelFormatter);
			}
			SetDataAdapter(model);
			ConfigureChartTitles(model, model.PlotAppearance?.TitleAppearance);
			DevExpress.Charts.Model.Legend legend = model.Legend;
			if (legend != null)
			{
				Legend legend2 = new Legend();
				ConfigureLegend(legend2, legend, model.LegendAppearance);
				HierarchicalControl.Legend = legend2;
			}
			else
			{
				HierarchicalControl.Legend = null;
			}
			HierarchicalControl.SelectionMode = SelectionMode.None;
			ConfigureCore(model);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapConfigurator : HierarchicalChartConfigurator
	{
		private TreeMapControl TreeMap => (TreeMapControl)base.HierarchicalControl;

		public TreeMapConfigurator(TreeMapControl treeMap)
			: base(treeMap)
		{
		}

		private TreeMapDataAdapterBase CreateFlatAdapter(HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter modelFlatDataAdapter, object dataSource)
		{
			TreeMapFlatDataAdapter treeMapFlatDataAdapter = new TreeMapFlatDataAdapter
			{
				LabelDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.LabelDataMember,
				ValueDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ValueDataMember,
				ColorDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ColorDataMember
			};
			if (modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers != null)
			{
				string[] groupDataMembers = modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers;
				foreach (string groupDataMember in groupDataMembers)
				{
					treeMapFlatDataAdapter.Groups.Add(new TreeMapGroupDefinition
					{
						GroupDataMember = groupDataMember
					});
				}
			}
			treeMapFlatDataAdapter.DataSource = dataSource;
			return treeMapFlatDataAdapter;
		}

		private TreeMapDataAdapterBase CreateHierarchicalAdapter(HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter modelHierarchicalDataAdapter, object dataSource)
		{
			TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter = new TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter();
			if (modelHierarchicalDataAdapter != null)
			{
				foreach (HierarchicalChartDataMapping mapping in modelHierarchicalDataAdapter.Mappings)
				{
					HierarchicalDataTemplate hierarchicalDataTemplate = new HierarchicalDataTemplate(mapping.Type);
					hierarchicalDataTemplate.ItemsSource = new Binding(mapping.ChildrenDataMember);
					treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter.ValueDataMember = mapping.ValueDataMember;
					TreeMap.Resources.Add(mapping.Type, hierarchicalDataTemplate);
				}
			}
			treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter.DataSource = dataSource;
			return treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter;
		}

		private HorizontalAlignment ConvertToHorizontalAlignment(StringAlignment labelHorizontalAlignment)
		{
			return labelHorizontalAlignment switch
			{
				StringAlignment.Center => HorizontalAlignment.Center, 
				StringAlignment.Far => HorizontalAlignment.Right, 
				_ => HorizontalAlignment.Left, 
			};
		}

		private VerticalAlignment ConvertToVerticalAlignment(StringAlignment labelHorizontalAlignment)
		{
			return labelHorizontalAlignment switch
			{
				StringAlignment.Center => VerticalAlignment.Center, 
				StringAlignment.Far => VerticalAlignment.Bottom, 
				_ => VerticalAlignment.Top, 
			};
		}

		private void ApplyLeafAppearance(TreeMapStyle style, HierarchicalItemAppearance itemAppearance)
		{
			if (itemAppearance == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool borderVisible = itemAppearance.BorderVisible;
			style.BorderThickness = (borderVisible ? new Thickness(1.0) : new Thickness(0.0));
			if (borderVisible)
			{
				style.BorderBrush = CreateSolidBrush(itemAppearance.BorderColor);
			}
			LabelAppearance labelAppearance = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance;
			if (labelAppearance == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			style.LeafLabelVisible = labelAppearance.Visible;
			if (labelAppearance.Visible)
			{
				style.LeafTextHorizontalAlignment = ConvertToHorizontalAlignment(labelAppearance.HorizontalAlignment);
				style.LeafTextVerticalAlignment = ConvertToVerticalAlignment(labelAppearance.VerticalAlignment);
				style.LeafForeground = CreateSolidBrush(labelAppearance.TextColor);
				if (labelAppearance.Font != null)
				{
					ConfigureFont(style, labelAppearance.Font);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ApplyGroupAppearance(TreeMapStyle style, HierarchicalItemAppearance itemAppearance)
		{
			if (itemAppearance == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (itemAppearance.BorderVisible)
			{
				style.GroupBorderBrush = CreateSolidBrush(itemAppearance.BorderColor);
			}
			style.GroupBackground = Brushes.Transparent;
			LabelAppearance labelAppearance = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance;
			if (labelAppearance == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			style.GroupLabelVisible = labelAppearance.Visible;
			if (!labelAppearance.Visible)
			{
				return;
			}
			style.GroupForeground = CreateSolidBrush(labelAppearance.TextColor);
			FontModel font = labelAppearance.Font;
			if (font != null)
			{
				style.GroupFontWeight = (font.Bold ? FontWeights.Bold : FontWeights.Regular);
				if (font.Italic)
				{
					style.GroupFontStyle = FontStyles.Italic;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(font.Name))
				{
					style.GroupFontFamily = new FontFamily(font.Name);
				}
				if (font.Size != 0f)
				{
					style.GroupFontSize = FontConversion.ConvertFromPointToPixel(font.Size);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void ConfigureCore(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			DevExpress.Charts.Model.TreeMap treeMapModel = (DevExpress.Charts.Model.TreeMap)model;
			TreeMapControl treeMap = TreeMap;
			treeMap.Loaded += delegate
			{
				TreeMapStyle treeMapStyle = new TreeMapStyle();
				ApplyLeafAppearance(treeMapStyle, treeMapModel.ItemAppearance);
				ApplyGroupAppearance(treeMapStyle, treeMapModel.GroupAppearance);
				treeMap.TreeMapStyle = treeMapStyle;
			};
			treeMap.EnableHighlighting = false;
		}

		protected override void SetColorizer(HierarchicalChart treeMapModel, CustomPalette palette, bool hasGroupData)
		{
			TreeMap.Colorizer = new TreeMapColorObjectColorizer
			{
				Palette = palette,
				ColorizeGroups = hasGroupData
			};
		}

		protected override void SetDataAdapter(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter = model.DataAdapter as HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter;
			TreeMap.DataAdapter = ((hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter != null) ? CreateFlatAdapter(hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter, model.DataSource) : CreateHierarchicalAdapter((HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter)model.DataAdapter, model.DataSource));
		}
	}
	internal class ModelSupportLabelFormatter : ILabelFormatter
	{
		private IDataLabelFormatter formatter;

		public ModelSupportLabelFormatter(IDataLabelFormatter formatter)
		{
			this.formatter = formatter;
		}

		public string GetDataLabelText(object sourceObject, object labelObject)
		{
			LabelPointData pointData = new LabelPointData(sourceObject, labelObject, double.NaN);
			return formatter.GetDataLabelText(pointData);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapColorObjectColorizer : TreeMapPaletteColorizer
	{
		public override Color? GetItemColor(TreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			object obj = item?.Color;
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return base.GetItemColor(item, group);
			}
			return ColorTranslator.ConvertToColor(obj);
		}
	}
	public static class ColorTranslator
	{
		public static Color? ConvertToColor(object color)
		{
			if (color is Color)
			{
				return (Color)color;
			}
			if (color is SolidColorBrush)
			{
				return ((SolidColorBrush)color).Color;
			}
			if (color is int)
			{
				byte[] bytes = BitConverter.GetBytes((int)color);
				return Color.FromArgb(bytes[3], bytes[2], bytes[1], bytes[0]);
			}
			if (color is uint)
			{
				byte[] bytes2 = BitConverter.GetBytes((uint)color);
				return Color.FromArgb(bytes2[3], bytes2[2], bytes2[1], bytes2[0]);
			}
			if (color is string)
			{
				string value = (string)color;
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
				{
					try
					{
						return (Color)ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(value);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public enum SelectionAction
	{
		None,
		Add,
		Remove,
		ReplaceAll
	}
	public static class SelectionHelper
	{
		public static SelectionAction CalculateSelectionAction(SelectionMode selectionMode, ModifierKeys keyModifiers, bool isItemSelected, bool isGroupItem)
		{
			switch (selectionMode)
			{
			case SelectionMode.Single:
				if (!isGroupItem)
				{
					return SelectionAction.ReplaceAll;
				}
				return SelectionAction.None;
			case SelectionMode.Multiple:
				if (!isItemSelected)
				{
					return SelectionAction.Add;
				}
				return SelectionAction.Remove;
			case SelectionMode.Extended:
				switch (keyModifiers)
				{
				case ModifierKeys.Shift:
					return SelectionAction.Add;
				case ModifierKeys.Control:
					if (!isItemSelected)
					{
						return SelectionAction.Add;
					}
					return SelectionAction.Remove;
				default:
					return SelectionAction.ReplaceAll;
				}
			default:
				return SelectionAction.None;
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class SelectedItemsControllerBase
	{
		private readonly List<TreeMapItem> selectedTreeMapItems = new List<TreeMapItem>();

		private readonly Locker updateSelectedItemLocker = new Locker();

		private readonly Locker updateSelectedItemsLocker = new Locker();

		private IList currentList;

		private CollectionChangedWeakEventHandler<SelectedItemsControllerBase> collectionChangedHandler;

		private CollectionChangedWeakEventHandler<SelectedItemsControllerBase> CollectionChangedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (collectionChangedHandler == null)
				{
					collectionChangedHandler = new CollectionChangedWeakEventHandler<SelectedItemsControllerBase>(this, (Action<SelectedItemsControllerBase, object, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs>)delegate(SelectedItemsControllerBase e, object o, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs args)
					{
						e.OnCollectionChanged(o, args);
					});
				}
				return collectionChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		protected HierarchicalChartControlBase HierarchicalControl { get; }

		public object SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				return HierarchicalControl.SelectedItem;
			}
			set
			{
				HierarchicalControl.SelectedItem = value;
			}
		}

		public IList SelectedItems
		{
			get
			{
				return HierarchicalControl.SelectedItems;
			}
			set
			{
				HierarchicalControl.SelectedItems = value;
			}
		}

		protected bool IsEventsSuspended => updateSelectedItemsLocker.IsLocked;

		public SelectedItemsControllerBase(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			HierarchicalControl = hierarchicalControl;
		}

		private void SafeSetSelectedItems(IList newList)
		{
			updateSelectedItemLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				SelectedItems = newList;
			}
			finally
			{
				updateSelectedItemLocker.Unlock();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateSelectedItemByList(IList list)
		{
			SelectedItem = ((list != null && list.Count > 0) ? list[0] : null);
		}

		private IList CopySelectedItems()
		{
			IList list = CreateNewSelectedItems();
			if (SelectedItems != null)
			{
				foreach (object selectedItem in SelectedItems)
				{
					list.Add(selectedItem);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private IList CreateNewSelectedItems()
		{
			return new ObservableCollection<object>();
		}

		private void EnsureSelectedItems()
		{
			if (SelectedItems == null)
			{
				SelectedItems = CreateNewSelectedItems();
			}
		}

		private void SetSelectedItem(object item)
		{
			EnsureSelectedItems();
			if (updateSelectedItemsLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				return;
			}
			IList list = CreateNewSelectedItems();
			if (item is IList list2)
			{
				foreach (object item2 in list2)
				{
					list.Add(item2);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			SafeSetSelectedItems(list);
		}

		private void AddSelectedItem(object item)
		{
			EnsureSelectedItems();
			IList list = CopySelectedItems();
			if (item is IList list2)
			{
				foreach (object item2 in list2)
				{
					if (!list.Contains(item2))
					{
						list.Add(item2);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (!list.Contains(item))
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			SafeSetSelectedItems(list);
		}

		private void RemoveSelectedItem(object item)
		{
			EnsureSelectedItems();
			IList list = CopySelectedItems();
			if (item is IList list2)
			{
				foreach (object item2 in list2)
				{
					list.Remove(item2);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				list.Remove(item);
			}
			SafeSetSelectedItems(list);
		}

		private void SuspendEvents()
		{
			updateSelectedItemsLocker.Lock();
		}

		private void ResumeEvents()
		{
			updateSelectedItemsLocker.Unlock();
		}

		private void SetCurrentList(IList list)
		{
			UnsubscribeListEvents(currentList);
			currentList = list;
			SubscribeListEvents(currentList);
		}

		private void SubscribeListEvents(object list)
		{
			if (list != null && list is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged += ((WeakEventHandler<SelectedItemsControllerBase, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler>)(object)CollectionChangedHandler).Handler;
			}
		}

		private void UnsubscribeListEvents(object list)
		{
			if (list != null && list is INotifyCollectionChanged notifyCollectionChanged)
			{
				notifyCollectionChanged.CollectionChanged -= ((WeakEventHandler<SelectedItemsControllerBase, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs, NotifyCollectionChangedEventHandler>)(object)CollectionChangedHandler).Handler;
			}
		}

		private void OnCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateActualItemsSelection();
		}

		protected abstract bool ContainsInSelectedItems(object source);

		internal abstract void UpdateActualItemsSelection();

		public void UpdateItemSelection(SelectionMode selectionMode, ModifierKeys keyModifiers, ISelectableItem item)
		{
			List<object> objectsToSelect = item.GetObjectsToSelect(this);
			switch (SelectionHelper.CalculateSelectionAction(selectionMode, keyModifiers, SelectedItems != null && ContainsInSelectedItems(objectsToSelect), objectsToSelect.Count > 1))
			{
			case SelectionAction.None:
				break;
			case SelectionAction.ReplaceAll:
				ClearSelectedItems();
				SetSelectedItem(objectsToSelect);
				break;
			case SelectionAction.Add:
				AddSelectedItem(objectsToSelect);
				break;
			case SelectionAction.Remove:
				RemoveSelectedItem(objectsToSelect);
				break;
			}
		}

		public void ClearSelectedItems()
		{
			if (SelectedItems != null)
			{
				IList newList = CreateNewSelectedItems();
				SafeSetSelectedItems(newList);
			}
		}

		public void OnUpdateSelectedItem(object item)
		{
			if (!updateSelectedItemLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				if (item == null)
				{
					SelectedItems = null;
				}
				else
				{
					SetSelectedItem(item);
				}
				UpdateActualItemsSelection();
			}
		}

		public void OnUpdateSelectedItems(IList oldList, IList newList)
		{
			SuspendEvents();
			try
			{
				UpdateSelectedItemByList(newList);
				SetCurrentList(newList);
			}
			finally
			{
				ResumeEvents();
				if ((oldList != null && oldList.Count > 0) || (newList != null && newList.Count > 0))
				{
					HierarchicalControl.RaiseSelectionChanged();
				}
			}
			UpdateActualItemsSelection();
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapSelectedItemsController : SelectedItemsControllerBase
	{
		public TreeMapSelectedItemsController(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
			: base(hierarchicalControl)
		{
		}

		private void UpdateActualItemsSelection(TreeMapItemCollection itemCollection)
		{
			if (itemCollection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in itemCollection)
			{
				if (item != null && !item.IsGroup)
				{
					object tag = item.Tag;
					item.IsSelected = base.SelectedItems != null && tag != null && ContainsInSelectedItems(tag);
				}
				if (item.Children.Count > 0)
				{
					UpdateActualItemsSelection(item.Children);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool ContainsInSelectedItems(object source)
		{
			if (source is IList list)
			{
				foreach (object item in list)
				{
					if (base.SelectedItems.Contains(item))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
			return base.SelectedItems.Contains(source);
		}

		internal override void UpdateActualItemsSelection()
		{
			UpdateActualItemsSelection(((TreeMapControl)base.HierarchicalControl).ActualItems);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstSelectedItemsController : SelectedItemsControllerBase
	{
		public SunburstSelectedItemsController(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
			: base(hierarchicalControl)
		{
		}

		protected override bool ContainsInSelectedItems(object source)
		{
			if (source is IList list)
			{
				foreach (object item in list)
				{
					if (!base.SelectedItems.Contains(item))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
			return base.SelectedItems.Contains(source);
		}

		internal override void UpdateActualItemsSelection()
		{
			SunburstControl sunburstControl = (SunburstControl)base.HierarchicalControl;
			if (!sunburstControl.HasSectorViewInfos)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo in sunburstControl.DataAdapter.SectorViewInfos)
			{
				object sourceObject = sectorViewInfo.SourceObject;
				sectorViewInfo.IsSelected = base.SelectedItems != null && sourceObject != null && ContainsInSelectedItems(sourceObject);
			}
		}
	}
	public static class DataHelper
	{
		public static IList GetList(object dataSource, string dataMember)
		{
			if (dataMember == null)
			{
				dataMember = string.Empty;
			}
			return MasterDetailHelper.GetDataSource(dataSource, dataMember);
		}
	}
	public static class LegendItemsCollector
	{
		private static void CollectLastLevelGroupsCore(List<IHierarchicalItem> result, IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> items)
		{
			foreach (IHierarchicalItem item in items)
			{
				IHierarchicalItem hierarchicalItem = item.Children.FirstOrDefault();
				if (hierarchicalItem == null || hierarchicalItem.Children.Count != 0)
				{
					CollectLastLevelGroupsCore(result, item.Children);
				}
				else if (item.Children.Count > 0)
				{
					result.Add(item);
				}
			}
		}

		public static IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> CollectLastLevelGroups(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> items)
		{
			List<IHierarchicalItem> result = new List<IHierarchicalItem>();
			CollectLastLevelGroupsCore(result, items);
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class SunburstHierarchicalDataController : ListSourceDataController, IDataControllerData
	{
		private readonly SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter dataAdapter;

		private Type previousDataType;

		public SunburstHierarchicalDataController(SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter dataAdapter)
		{
			((DataControllerBase)this).DataClient = (IDataControllerData)(object)this;
			this.dataAdapter = dataAdapter;
		}

		UnboundColumnInfoCollection IDataControllerData.GetUnboundColumns()
		{
			return null;
		}

		object IDataControllerData.GetUnboundData(int row, DataColumnInfo col, object value)
		{
			return null;
		}

		void IDataControllerData.SetUnboundData(int row, DataColumnInfo col, object value)
		{
		}

		private void LoadItems(IList<IHierarchicalItem> items, IList<SunburstDataMapping> mappings, int level, Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos> columns)
		{
			Dictionary<IHierarchicalItem, IList> dictionary = null;
			level++;
			int listSourceRowCount = ((DataControllerBase)this).ListSourceRowCount;
			for (int i = 0; i < listSourceRowCount; i++)
			{
				object row = ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRow(i, (OperationCompleted)null, false);
				if (row == null || !GetColumns(columns, GetMappingByType(row.GetType(), mappings), out var valueColumn, out var labelColumn, out var colorColumn, out var childrenColumn))
				{
					continue;
				}
				object value = ((valueColumn != null) ? ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValue(i, valueColumn, (OperationCompleted)null, false) : null);
				double value2 = Convert.ToDouble(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				object label = ((labelColumn != null) ? ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValue(i, labelColumn, (OperationCompleted)null, false) : null);
				object color = ((colorColumn != null) ? ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValue(i, colorColumn, (OperationCompleted)null, false) : null);
				TreeMapItem treeMapItem = new TreeMapItem(value2, label, row, color, -1);
				items.Add(treeMapItem);
				if (childrenColumn == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				IList dataSource = MasterDetailHelper.GetDataSource(((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValueDetail(i, childrenColumn), (string)null);
				if (dataSource != null && dataSource.Count > 0)
				{
					if (dictionary == null)
					{
						dictionary = new Dictionary<IHierarchicalItem, IList>();
					}
					dictionary.Add(treeMapItem, dataSource);
				}
			}
			if (dictionary == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<IHierarchicalItem, IList> item in dictionary)
			{
				UpdateData(item.Value, item.Key.Children, mappings, level, columns);
			}
		}

		private bool GetColumns(Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos> columnsCache, SunburstDataMapping mapping, out DataColumnInfo valueColumn, out DataColumnInfo labelColumn, out DataColumnInfo colorColumn, out DataColumnInfo childrenColumn)
		{
			if (mapping == null)
			{
				valueColumn = null;
				labelColumn = null;
				childrenColumn = null;
				colorColumn = null;
				return false;
			}
			if (!columnsCache.TryGetValue(mapping.Type, out var value))
			{
				valueColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.ValueDataMember);
				labelColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.LabelDataMember);
				childrenColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.ChildrenDataMember);
				colorColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.ColorDataMember);
				columnsCache.Add(mapping.Type, new ColumnInfos(valueColumn, labelColumn, colorColumn, childrenColumn));
			}
			else
			{
				valueColumn = value.ValueColumn;
				labelColumn = value.LabelColumn;
				colorColumn = value.ColorColumn;
				childrenColumn = value.ChildrenColumn;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private DataColumnInfo GetColumnInfo(string dataMember)
		{
			DataColumnInfo val = ((DataControllerBase)this).Columns[dataMember];
			return val ?? ((DataControllerBase)this).DetailColumns[dataMember];
		}

		private SunburstDataMapping GetMappingByType(Type type, IList<SunburstDataMapping> mappings)
		{
			foreach (SunburstDataMapping mapping in mappings)
			{
				if (type == mapping.Type)
				{
					return mapping;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override void OnListSourceChanged()
		{
			Type type = ((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource[0].GetType();
			if (!(previousDataType == type))
			{
				previousDataType = type;
				((DataControllerBase)this).OnListSourceChanged();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnBindingListChanged(ListChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((DataController)this).OnBindingListChanged(e);
			dataAdapter.UpdateData();
		}

		public void UpdateData(IList listSource, IList<IHierarchicalItem> items, IList<SunburstDataMapping> mappings, int level, Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos> columns)
		{
			if (((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource != null)
			{
				((DataControllerBase)this).UnsubscribeListChanged(((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.NotificationProvider, (object)((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource);
			}
			((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource = listSource;
			if (listSource != null)
			{
				((DataControllerBase)this).SubscribeListChanged(((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.NotificationProvider, (object)listSource);
			}
			LoadItems(items, mappings, level, columns);
		}
	}
	public class ColumnInfos
	{
		public DataColumnInfo ValueColumn { get; }

		public DataColumnInfo LabelColumn { get; }

		public DataColumnInfo ChildrenColumn { get; }

		public DataColumnInfo ColorColumn { get; }

		public ColumnInfos(DataColumnInfo valueColumn, DataColumnInfo labelColumn, DataColumnInfo colorColumn, DataColumnInfo childrenColumn)
		{
			ValueColumn = valueColumn;
			LabelColumn = labelColumn;
			ChildrenColumn = childrenColumn;
			ColorColumn = colorColumn;
		}
	}
	internal class GroupDataMembersTypeConverter : TypeConverter
	{
		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			if (sourceType == typeof(string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertFrom(context, sourceType);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			if (value is string)
			{
				ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				string[] array = ((string)value).Split(',');
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(array[i].Trim());
				}
				return observableCollection;
			}
			return base.ConvertFrom(context, culture, value);
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == typeof(string) && value is ObservableCollection<string>)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = (ObservableCollection<string>)value;
				for (int i = 0; i < observableCollection.Count - 1; i++)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(observableCollection[i].Trim());
					stringBuilder.Append(", ");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(observableCollection[observableCollection.Count - 1].Trim());
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == typeof(string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstItemPatternProvider : PatternDataProvider
	{
		private SunburstSectorViewInfo Item => (SunburstSectorViewInfo)base.Context;

		public SunburstItemPatternProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
			: base(patternConstant)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CheckContext(object value)
		{
			return value is SunburstSectorViewInfo;
		}

		protected override object GetValue()
		{
			if (Item != null)
			{
				switch (base.PatternConstant)
				{
				case PatternConstants.Label:
					return Item.TextObject;
				case PatternConstants.Value:
					return Item.Value;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	{
		private static Brush defaultStroke;

		private static Brush defaultForeground;

		private static Brush defaultFill;

		private static DropShadowEffect defaultShadow;

		private const int DefaultStrokeThickness = 1;

		private readonly IHierarchicalItem item;

		private double startAngleRad;

		private double endAngleRad;

		private static Brush DefaultStroke
		{
			get
			{
				if (defaultStroke == null)
				{
					defaultStroke = CreateBrush(Color.FromArgb(75, 0, 0, 0));
				}
				return defaultStroke;
			}
		}

		private static Brush DefaultForeground
		{
			get
			{
				if (defaultForeground == null)
				{
					defaultForeground = CreateBrush(Colors.White);
				}
				return defaultForeground;
			}
		}

		private static Brush DefaultFill
		{
			get
			{
				if (defaultFill == null)
				{
					defaultFill = CreateBrush(Colors.LightGray);
				}
				return defaultStroke;
			}
		}

		private static DropShadowEffect DefaultShadow
		{
			get
			{
				if (defaultShadow == null)
				{
					defaultShadow = new DropShadowEffect
					{
						Color = Colors.Black,
						BlurRadius = 5.0,
						RenderingBias = RenderingBias.Quality,
						ShadowDepth = 0.0,
						Opacity = 1.0
					};
					defaultShadow.Freeze();
				}
				return defaultShadow;
			}
		}

		{
			get
			{
				return startAngleRad;
			}
			internal set
			{
				if (value != startAngleRad)
				{
					startAngleRad = value;
					Geometry = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public double EndAngleRad
		{
			get
			{
				return endAngleRad;
			}
			internal set
			{
				if (value != endAngleRad)
				{
					endAngleRad = value;
					Geometry = null;
				}
			}
		}

		bool IToolTipItem.IsGroup => GroupInfo.IsParent;

		string ISunburstSectorInfo.Text => ActualText;

		string ILegendSourceItem.Text => ActualText;

		object ILegendSourceItem.Brush => Fill;

		private static Brush CreateBrush(Color color)
		{
			Brush brush = new SolidColorBrush(color);
			brush.Freeze();
			return brush;
		}

		public SunburstSectorViewInfo(IHierarchicalItem item, double startAngleRad, double endAngleRad, object text, SunburstItemGroupInfo groupInfo, object dataSourceColor)
		{
			this.item = item;
			DataSourceColor = dataSourceColor;
			StartAngleRad = startAngleRad;
			EndAngleRad = endAngleRad;
			TextObject = text;
			GroupInfo = groupInfo;
			Stroke = DefaultStroke;
			StrokeThickness = 1.0;
			Fill = DefaultFill;
			Foreground = DefaultForeground;
			FontStyle = FontStyles.Normal;
			FontWeight = FontWeights.Normal;
			FontSize = SystemFonts.IconFontSize;
			FontFamily = SystemFonts.CaptionFontFamily;
			LabelVisible = true;
			Shadow = DefaultShadow;
		}

		List<object> ISelectableItem.GetObjectsToSelect(SelectedItemsControllerBase selectionController)
		{
			if (!(SourceObject is IEnumerable<object> collection))
			{
				return new List<object> { SourceObject };
			}
			return new List<object>(collection);
		}

		private bool IsDoubleEquals(double d1, double d2)
		{
			return Math.Round(d1 - d2, 10) == 0.0;
		}

		public bool IsInRange(SunburstSectorViewInfo item)
		{
			if (StartAngleRad > item.StartAngleRad || IsDoubleEquals(StartAngleRad, item.StartAngleRad))
			{
				if (!(EndAngleRad < item.EndAngleRad))
				{
					return IsDoubleEquals(EndAngleRad, item.EndAngleRad);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal static class SunburstLayoutCalculator
	{
		public const double DoublePI = Math.PI * 2.0;

		private static Rect CalcHorizontalLabelBounds(Size minSize, double startAngleRad, double endAngleRad, Point center, double minRadius, double maxRadius)
		{
			Tuple<TreeMapRect, TreeMapRect> tuple = LabelBoundsCalculator.CalcHorizontalLabelBounds(startAngleRad, endAngleRad, center.X, center.Y, minRadius, maxRadius, 20.0);
			Rect result = tuple.Item1.ToRect();
			Rect result2 = tuple.Item2.ToRect();
			if (result.Width < minSize.Width || result.Height < minSize.Height)
			{
				return result2;
			}
			if (!(result.Width * result.Height > result2.Width * result2.Height))
			{
				return result2;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static Size CalcTangentPositionSmallAngleSize(double minRadius, double maxRadius, double angleDiff)
		{
			double num = Math.Abs(2.0 * Math.Tan(angleDiff) * minRadius * 1.2);
			double num2 = Math.Asin(num / (2.0 * maxRadius));
			double num3 = Math.Cos(num2 / 2.0) * maxRadius;
			return new Size(num, Math.Max(num3 - minRadius, 0.0));
		}

		private static Size CalcTangentPositionLargeAngleSize(double minRadius, double maxRadius, double segmentBreadth, double sweepAngleRad)
		{
			double num = segmentBreadth / 2.0;
			double num2 = minRadius + (segmentBreadth - num) / 2.0;
			double num3 = num2 + num;
			double num4 = 2.0 * Math.Sqrt(maxRadius * maxRadius - num3 * num3);
			if (sweepAngleRad == Math.PI * 2.0)
			{
				return new Size(num4, num);
			}
			double val = Math.Abs(2.0 * num2 * Math.Tan(sweepAngleRad / 2.0));
			return new Size(Math.Min(val, num4), num);
		}

		private static Size CalcRadialPositionSize(double minRadius, double maxRadius, double segmentBreadth, double sweepAngleRad)
		{
			double width = segmentBreadth * 3.0 / 4.0;
			double num = maxRadius - segmentBreadth / 8.0;
			double num2 = Math.Acos(num / maxRadius);
			double val = ((num2 * 2.0 < sweepAngleRad) ? (Math.Sqrt(maxRadius * maxRadius - num * num) * 2.0) : (num * Math.Tan(sweepAngleRad / 2.0) * 2.0));
			double num3 = minRadius + segmentBreadth / 8.0;
			double num4 = Math.Acos(num3 / maxRadius);
			double val2 = ((num4 * 2.0 < sweepAngleRad) ? (Math.Sqrt(maxRadius * maxRadius - num3 * num3) * 2.0) : (num3 * Math.Abs(Math.Tan(sweepAngleRad / 2.0) * 2.0)));
			return new Size(width, Math.Min(val, val2));
		}

		private static void CalculateLabelLayout(SunburstSectorViewInfo sector, Point center, SunburstLabelDisplayMode mode, double startAngle, double endAngle, Size minLabelSize)
		{
			double minRadius = sector.MinRadius;
			double maxRadius = sector.MaxRadius;
			Rect rect;
			if (mode == SunburstLabelDisplayMode.Horizontal)
			{
				rect = CalcHorizontalLabelBounds(minLabelSize, startAngle, endAngle, center, minRadius, maxRadius);
				sector.LabelTransform = new RotateTransform(0.0);
			}
			else
			{
				double num = (startAngle + endAngle) / 2.0;
				double num2 = TrigonometricUtils.Radian2Degree(num);
				double num3 = Math.Abs(endAngle - startAngle);
				double num4 = maxRadius - minRadius;
				Size size;
				if (mode == SunburstLabelDisplayMode.Radial)
				{
					size = CalcRadialPositionSize(minRadius, maxRadius, num4, num3);
					RotateTransform labelTransform = (sector.LabelTransform = new RotateTransform((num2 > 90.0 && num2 < 270.0) ? (num2 - 180.0) : num2));
					sector.LabelTransform = labelTransform;
				}
				else
				{
					size = ((!(num3 < Math.PI / 4.0)) ? CalcTangentPositionLargeAngleSize(minRadius, maxRadius, num4, num3) : CalcTangentPositionSmallAngleSize(minRadius, maxRadius, endAngle - num));
					RotateTransform labelTransform = (sector.LabelTransform = new RotateTransform((num2 > 180.0) ? (num2 + 90.0) : (num2 - 90.0)));
					sector.LabelTransform = labelTransform;
				}
				Point point = center.MoveByAngle(minRadius + num4 / 2.0, num);
				double x = point.X - size.Width / 2.0;
				double y = point.Y - size.Height / 2.0;
				rect = new Rect(x, y, size.Width, size.Height);
			}
			sector.LabelBounds = ((rect.Width > minLabelSize.Width && rect.Height > minLabelSize.Height) ? rect : new Rect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
		}

		public static void Calculate(IList<SunburstSectorViewInfo> sectorViewInfos, Size availableSize, int levelCount, double holeRadiusPercent, SunburstLabelDisplayMode labelDisplayMode, Size minLabelSize)
		{
			Point point = new Point(availableSize.Width / 2.0, availableSize.Height / 2.0);
			double num = Math.Min(availableSize.Height, availableSize.Width) / 2.0;
			double num2 = num * (holeRadiusPercent / 100.0);
			double num3 = (num - num2) / (double)levelCount;
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo in sectorViewInfos)
			{
				double num4 = (sectorViewInfo.MinRadius = num2 + num3 * (double)sectorViewInfo.ZeroBasedLevel);
				double num6 = (sectorViewInfo.MaxRadius = num4 + num3);
				double num8 = Math.PI * 2.0 - sectorViewInfo.StartAngleRad;
				Point point2 = point.MoveByAngle(num4, num8);
				PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure
				{
					IsClosed = true
				};
				pathFigure.StartPoint = point2;
				pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment
				{
					Point = point.MoveByAngle(num6, num8)
				});
				double num9 = Math.PI * 2.0 - sectorViewInfo.EndAngleRad;
				double num10 = Math.Abs(num9 - num8);
				if (num10 > Math.PI)
				{
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new ArcSegment
					{
						SweepDirection = SweepDirection.Counterclockwise,
						IsLargeArc = true,
						Size = new Size(num6, num6),
						Point = point.MoveByAngle(num6, num8 + Math.PI)
					});
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new ArcSegment
					{
						SweepDirection = SweepDirection.Counterclockwise,
						Size = new Size(num6, num6),
						Point = point.MoveByAngle(num6, num9)
					});
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment
					{
						Point = point.MoveByAngle(num4, num9)
					});
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new ArcSegment
					{
						SweepDirection = SweepDirection.Clockwise,
						Size = new Size(num4, num4),
						Point = point.MoveByAngle(num4, num8 + Math.PI)
					});
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new ArcSegment
					{
						SweepDirection = SweepDirection.Clockwise,
						IsLargeArc = true,
						Size = new Size(num4, num4),
						Point = point2
					});
				}
				else
				{
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new ArcSegment
					{
						SweepDirection = SweepDirection.Counterclockwise,
						Size = new Size(num6, num6),
						Point = point.MoveByAngle(num6, num9)
					});
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment
					{
						Point = point.MoveByAngle(num4, num9)
					});
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new ArcSegment
					{
						SweepDirection = SweepDirection.Clockwise,
						Size = new Size(num4, num4),
						Point = point2
					});
				}
				PathGeometry pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
				pathGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure);
				sectorViewInfo.Geometry = pathGeometry;
				CalculateLabelLayout(sectorViewInfo, point, labelDisplayMode, num8, num9, minLabelSize);
			}
		}

		public static Rect CalculateHoleContentBounds(Size constraint, double holeRadiusPercent)
		{
			double num = Math.Min(constraint.Width, constraint.Height);
			double num2 = num / 2.0 * (holeRadiusPercent / 100.0);
			double num3 = 2.0 * num2 / Math.Sqrt(2.0);
			double x = constraint.Width / 2.0 - 0.5 * num3;
			double y = constraint.Height / 2.0 - 0.5 * num3;
			return new Rect(x, y, num3, num3);
		}
	}
	public enum ToolTipNavigationAction
	{
		MouseUp,
		MouseMove,
		MouseLeave
	}
	public class ToolTipController
	{
		private readonly HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl;

		private readonly DispatcherTimer initialTimer = new DispatcherTimer();

		private readonly DispatcherTimer popTimer = new DispatcherTimer();

		private Point mousePosition;

		private IHitTestableElement previousElement;

		private IHitTestableElement hitElement;

		private bool HitItemIsGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (HitItem != null)
				{
					return HitItem.IsGroup;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool ElementChanged => !object.Equals(hitElement, previousElement);

		private bool CloseOnClick => Options.CloseOnClick;

		private ToolTipOptions Options => hierarchicalControl.ActualToolTipOptions;

		private ToolTipOpenMode OpenMode => Options.OpenMode;

		private ToolTipInfo Info => hierarchicalControl.ToolTipInfo;

		private IToolTipItem HitItem => hitElement?.Element as IToolTipItem;

		public ToolTipController(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
		{
			this.hierarchicalControl = hierarchicalControl;
			initialTimer.Tick += InitialTimerTick;
			popTimer.Tick += PopTimerTick;
		}

		private void InitialTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ShowToolTip();
		}

		private void PopTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			HideToolTip(OpenMode == ToolTipOpenMode.OnClick);
		}

		private string CreateToolTipText()
		{
			PatternParser patternParser = new PatternParser(HitItemIsGroup ? hierarchicalControl.ToolTipGroupPattern : hierarchicalControl.ToolTipPattern, Info);
			patternParser.SetContext(HitItem);
			return patternParser.GetText();
		}

		private void ShowToolTip()
		{
			initialTimer.Stop();
			if (hitElement != null)
			{
				Point controlAnchorPosition = Options.ActualPosition.CalculateToolTipPoint(mousePosition, (FrameworkElement)hitElement, hierarchicalControl);
				Info.Layout = ToolTipLayoutHelper.CalculateLayout(controlAnchorPosition, hierarchicalControl);
				Info.ToolTipText = CreateToolTipText();
				Info.ContentTemplate = (HitItemIsGroup ? hierarchicalControl.ToolTipGroupContentTemplate : hierarchicalControl.ToolTipContentTemplate);
				Info.Item = HitItem?.SourceObject;
				Info.Visible = true;
				RunTimer(popTimer, Options.AutoPopDelay);
			}
		}

		private bool RunTimer(DispatcherTimer timer, TimeSpan interval)
		{
			if (interval.TotalMilliseconds > 0.0)
			{
				timer.Interval = interval;
				timer.Start();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void CreateToolTip()
		{
			if (hitElement != null && hierarchicalControl.ToolTipEnabled)
			{
				if (ElementChanged)
				{
					HideToolTip(resetLastElement: true);
					previousElement = hitElement;
					TimeSpan interval = ((hierarchicalControl.ToolTipOptions != null) ? hierarchicalControl.ToolTipOptions.InitialDelay : TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(0.0));
					if (!RunTimer(initialTimer, interval))
					{
						ShowToolTip();
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				HideToolTip(resetLastElement: true);
			}
		}

		private void HideToolTip(bool resetLastElement)
		{
			if (resetLastElement)
			{
				previousElement = null;
			}
			initialTimer.Stop();
			popTimer.Stop();
			Info.Visible = false;
		}

		private void ProcessToolTipOnHover(ToolTipNavigationAction navigationAction)
		{
			switch (navigationAction)
			{
			case ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseUp:
				if (CloseOnClick)
				{
					HideToolTip(resetLastElement: false);
				}
				break;
			case ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseMove:
				CreateToolTip();
				break;
			case ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseLeave:
				HideToolTip(resetLastElement: true);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void ProcessToolTipOnClick(ToolTipNavigationAction navigationAction)
		{
			switch (navigationAction)
			{
			case ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseUp:
				if (!Info.Visible || ElementChanged)
				{
					CreateToolTip();
				}
				else if (CloseOnClick)
				{
					HideToolTip(resetLastElement: true);
				}
				break;
			case ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseMove:
				if (ElementChanged)
				{
					HideToolTip(resetLastElement: true);
				}
				break;
			case ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseLeave:
				HideToolTip(resetLastElement: true);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateToolTip(Point position, ToolTipNavigationAction navigationAction, IHitTestableElement hitElement)
		{
			mousePosition = position;
			if (hierarchicalControl.ToolTipEnabled)
			{
				this.hitElement = hitElement;
				if (OpenMode == ToolTipOpenMode.OnClick)
				{
					ProcessToolTipOnClick(navigationAction);
				}
				else
				{
					ProcessToolTipOnHover(navigationAction);
				}
			}
			else if (Info.Visible)
			{
				HideToolTip(resetLastElement: true);
			}
		}
	}
	public static class ToolTipLayoutHelper
	{
		private static Point CalculateLocalScreenPosition(Point controlAnchorPosition, FrameworkElement targetElement)
		{
			if (targetElement.FlowDirection == FlowDirection.RightToLeft)
			{
				controlAnchorPosition = new Point(targetElement.ActualWidth - controlAnchorPosition.X, controlAnchorPosition.Y);
			}
			Point point = ScreenHelper.GetScreenPoint(targetElement, default(Point));
			if (targetElement.FlowDirection == FlowDirection.RightToLeft)
			{
				point = new Point(point.X - targetElement.ActualWidth, point.Y);
			}
			Point point2 = new Point(point.X + controlAnchorPosition.X, point.Y + controlAnchorPosition.Y);
			return new Point(point2.X, point2.Y);
		}

		public static ToolTipLayout CalculateLayout(Point controlAnchorPosition, FrameworkElement targetElement)
		{
			Point controlAnchorPosition2 = CorrectPointByTransform(controlAnchorPosition, targetElement);
			Point point = CalculateLocalScreenPosition(controlAnchorPosition2, targetElement);
			Rect screenRect = ScreenHelper.GetScreenRect(point);
			if (screenRect.Width == 0.0)
			{
				Point screenPoint = ScreenHelper.GetScreenPoint(targetElement, default(Point));
				Point position = Mouse.GetPosition(targetElement);
				Point point2 = new Point(screenPoint.X + position.X, screenPoint.Y + position.Y);
				screenRect = ScreenHelper.GetScreenRect(point2);
			}
			if (point.X < screenRect.Left)
			{
				point.X = screenRect.Left;
			}
			return new ToolTipLayout(point, screenRect);
		}

		private static Point CorrectPointByTransform(Point point, FrameworkElement targetElement)
		{
			Point point2 = targetElement.PointToScreen(new Point(0.0, 0.0));
			Point point3 = targetElement.PointToScreen(new Point(targetElement.ActualWidth, targetElement.ActualHeight));
			double num = Math.Abs(point3.X - point2.X) / targetElement.ActualWidth;
			double num2 = Math.Abs(point3.Y - point2.Y) / targetElement.ActualHeight;
			return ScreenHelper.GetScaledPoint(new Point(point.X * num, point.Y * num2));
		}

		public static Size CorrectSizeByTransform(Size size, FrameworkElement targetElement)
		{
			Point point = CorrectPointByTransform(new Point(0.0, 0.0), targetElement);
			Point point2 = CorrectPointByTransform(new Point(size.Width, size.Height), targetElement);
			return new Size(point2.X - point.X, point2.Y - point.Y);
		}
	}
	public class ToolTipLayout
	{
		private readonly Point anchorPoint;

		private readonly Rect screenBounds;

		public Point AnchorPoint => anchorPoint;

		public Rect ScreenBounds => screenBounds;

		public ToolTipLayout(Point anchorPoint, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			this.anchorPoint = anchorPoint;
			this.screenBounds = screenBounds;
		}
	}
	public abstract class ToolTipLayoutStrategy
	{
		protected abstract HorizontalAlignment LeftToolTipAlignment { get; }

		protected abstract HorizontalAlignment RightToolTipAlignment { get; }

		public static ToolTipLayoutStrategy Create(FlowDirection flowDirection)
		{
			if (flowDirection == FlowDirection.LeftToRight)
			{
				return new LeftToRightLayoutStrategy();
			}
			return new RightToLeftLayoutStrategy();
		}

		private double GetHorizontalShadowOffset(double width, HorizontalAlignment hBeakAlignment)
		{
			if (hBeakAlignment != LeftToolTipAlignment)
			{
				return width;
			}
			return 0.0 - width;
		}

		private double GetVerticalShadowOffset(double height, VerticalAlignment vBeakAlignment)
		{
			if (vBeakAlignment != VerticalAlignment.Top)
			{
				return height;
			}
			return 0.0 - height;
		}

		protected abstract double GetCenterOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size);

		protected abstract double GetLeftOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size);

		protected abstract double GetRightOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size);

		protected abstract bool IsLeftOverflow(double offset, Size size, Rect screenBounds);

		protected abstract bool IsRightOverflow(double offset, Size size, Rect screenBounds);

		protected bool IsTopOverflow(double offset, Size size, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			return offset < screenBounds.Top;
		}

		protected double GetBottomOffsetY(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.Y;
		}

		protected double GetTopOffsetY(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.Y - size.Height;
		}

		public Point CalculateToolTipPosition(Point anchorPoint, Size toolTipSize, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			double num = GetCenterOffsetX(anchorPoint, toolTipSize);
			if (IsLeftOverflow(num, toolTipSize, screenBounds))
			{
				num = GetLeftOffsetX(anchorPoint, toolTipSize);
			}
			else if (IsRightOverflow(num, toolTipSize, screenBounds))
			{
				num = GetRightOffsetX(anchorPoint, toolTipSize);
			}
			double num2 = GetTopOffsetY(anchorPoint, toolTipSize);
			if (IsTopOverflow(num2, toolTipSize, screenBounds))
			{
				num2 = GetBottomOffsetY(anchorPoint, toolTipSize);
			}
			return new Point(num, num2);
		}

		public HorizontalAlignment CalculateHorizontalBeakPosition(Point anchorPoint, Size toolTipSize, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			double centerOffsetX = GetCenterOffsetX(anchorPoint, toolTipSize);
			if (IsLeftOverflow(centerOffsetX, toolTipSize, screenBounds))
			{
				return LeftToolTipAlignment;
			}
			if (IsRightOverflow(centerOffsetX, toolTipSize, screenBounds))
			{
				return RightToolTipAlignment;
			}
			return HorizontalAlignment.Center;
		}

		public VerticalAlignment CalculateVerticalBeakPosition(Point anchorPoint, Size toolTipSize, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			double topOffsetY = GetTopOffsetY(anchorPoint, toolTipSize);
			if (IsTopOverflow(topOffsetY, toolTipSize, screenBounds))
			{
				return VerticalAlignment.Top;
			}
			return VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
		}

		public Point CorrectPositionByShadow(Point anchorPoint, FrameworkElement target, VerticalAlignment vBeakAlignment, HorizontalAlignment hBeakAlignment, Thickness shadowPadding)
		{
			double width = 0.0;
			if (hBeakAlignment == LeftToolTipAlignment)
			{
				width = shadowPadding.Left;
			}
			else if (hBeakAlignment == RightToolTipAlignment)
			{
				width = shadowPadding.Right;
			}
			double height = 0.0;
			switch (vBeakAlignment)
			{
			case VerticalAlignment.Top:
				height = shadowPadding.Top;
				break;
			case VerticalAlignment.Bottom:
				height = shadowPadding.Bottom;
				break;
			}
			Size size = ToolTipLayoutHelper.CorrectSizeByTransform(new Size(width, height), target);
			width = GetHorizontalShadowOffset(size.Width, hBeakAlignment);
			height = GetVerticalShadowOffset(size.Height, vBeakAlignment);
			return new Point(anchorPoint.X + width, anchorPoint.Y + height);
		}
	}
	public class LeftToRightLayoutStrategy : ToolTipLayoutStrategy
	{
		protected override HorizontalAlignment LeftToolTipAlignment => HorizontalAlignment.Left;

		protected override HorizontalAlignment RightToolTipAlignment => HorizontalAlignment.Right;

		protected override double GetCenterOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.X - 0.5 * size.Width;
		}

		protected override double GetLeftOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.X;
		}

		protected override bool IsLeftOverflow(double offset, Size size, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			return offset < screenBounds.Left;
		}

		protected override double GetRightOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.X - size.Width;
		}

		protected override bool IsRightOverflow(double offset, Size size, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			return offset + size.Width > screenBounds.Right;
		}
	}
	public class RightToLeftLayoutStrategy : ToolTipLayoutStrategy
	{
		protected override HorizontalAlignment LeftToolTipAlignment => HorizontalAlignment.Right;

		protected override HorizontalAlignment RightToolTipAlignment => HorizontalAlignment.Left;

		protected override double GetCenterOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.X + 0.5 * size.Width;
		}

		protected override double GetLeftOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.X + size.Width;
		}

		protected override bool IsLeftOverflow(double offset, Size size, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			return offset - size.Width < screenBounds.Left;
		}

		protected override double GetRightOffsetX(Point anchorPoint, Size size)
		{
			return anchorPoint.X;
		}

		protected override bool IsRightOverflow(double offset, Size size, Rect screenBounds)
		{
			return offset > screenBounds.Right;
		}
	}
	public abstract class ToolTipRelativePositionBase
	{
		public abstract Point CalculateToolTipPoint(Rect itemRect, FrameworkElement itemPresentation, Point origin);
	}
	public class TreeMapToolTipRelativePosition : ToolTipRelativePositionBase
	{
		public override Point CalculateToolTipPoint(Rect itemRect, FrameworkElement itemPresentation, Point origin)
		{
			return new Point(itemRect.Left + itemRect.Width * origin.X, itemRect.Top + itemRect.Height * origin.Y);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstToolTipRelativePosition : ToolTipRelativePositionBase
	{
		public override Point CalculateToolTipPoint(Rect itemRect, FrameworkElement itemPresentation, Point origin)
		{
			SunburstSectorViewInfo sectorViewInfo = ((SunburstSectorPresentation)itemPresentation).SectorViewInfo;
			double angleRad = Math.PI * 2.0 - (sectorViewInfo.StartAngleRad + (sectorViewInfo.EndAngleRad - sectorViewInfo.StartAngleRad) * origin.X);
			double length = sectorViewInfo.MinRadius + (sectorViewInfo.MaxRadius - sectorViewInfo.MinRadius) * origin.Y;
			return RectExtension.Center(itemRect).MoveByAngle(length, angleRad);
		}
	}
	public static class SelectionAndHighlightingVisualHelper
	{
		private static byte selectionAlpha = 130;

		private static byte highlightingAlpha = 180;

		private static Point endPoint = new Point(3.0, 3.0);

		private static LinearGradientBrush selectionMask;

		private static LinearGradientBrush highlightingMask;

		private static LinearGradientBrush GetBrush(byte alpha)
		{
			LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush = new LinearGradientBrush
			{
				SpreadMethod = GradientSpreadMethod.Repeat,
				EndPoint = endPoint,
				MappingMode = BrushMappingMode.Absolute
			};
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = Color.FromArgb(alpha, 0, 0, 0),
				Offset = 0.5
			});
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = Colors.Black,
				Offset = 0.5
			});
			return linearGradientBrush;
		}

		private static LinearGradientBrush GetBrush(byte alpha, Color color)
		{
			LinearGradientBrush linearGradientBrush = new LinearGradientBrush
			{
				SpreadMethod = GradientSpreadMethod.Repeat,
				EndPoint = endPoint,
				MappingMode = BrushMappingMode.Absolute
			};
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = Color.FromArgb(alpha, color.R, color.G, color.B),
				Offset = 0.5
			});
			linearGradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(new GradientStop
			{
				Color = color,
				Offset = 0.5
			});
			return linearGradientBrush;
		}

		public static LinearGradientBrush GetSelectionBrush()
		{
			if (selectionMask == null)
			{
				selectionMask = GetBrush(selectionAlpha);
			}
			return selectionMask;
		}

		public static LinearGradientBrush GetHighlightingBrush()
		{
			if (highlightingMask == null)
			{
				highlightingMask = GetBrush(highlightingAlpha);
			}
			return highlightingMask;
		}

		public static LinearGradientBrush GetSelectionBrush(Color color)
		{
			LinearGradientBrush brush = GetBrush(selectionAlpha, color);
			brush.Freeze();
			return brush;
		}

		public static LinearGradientBrush GetHighlightingBrush(Color color)
		{
			LinearGradientBrush brush = GetBrush(highlightingAlpha, color);
			brush.Freeze();
			return brush;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemCollectionDecorator : IList<IHierarchicalItem>, ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>, IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem>, IEnumerable
	{
		private readonly TreeMapItemCollection collection;

		bool ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.IsReadOnly => false;

		int ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Count => collection.Count;

		IHierarchicalItem IList<IHierarchicalItem>.this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return collection[index];
			}
			set
			{
				if (value is TreeMapItem value2)
				{
					collection[index] = value2;
				}
			}
		}

		IEnumerator<IHierarchicalItem> IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem>.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return collection.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return collection.GetEnumerator();
		}

		void ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Add(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			if (item is TreeMapItem value)
			{
				collection.Add(value);
			}
		}

		bool ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Remove(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			if (item is TreeMapItem value)
			{
				return collection.Remove(value);
			}
			return false;
		}

		void ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Clear()
		{
			collection.Clear();
		}

		bool ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Contains(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			if (item is TreeMapItem value)
			{
				return collection.Contains(value);
			}
			return false;
		}

		void ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.CopyTo(IHierarchicalItem[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in collection)
			{
				array[arrayIndex++] = item;
			}
		}

		void IList<IHierarchicalItem>.Insert(int index, IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			if (item is TreeMapItem value)
			{
				collection.Insert(index, value);
			}
		}

		void IList<IHierarchicalItem>.RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			collection.RemoveAt(index);
		}

		int IList<IHierarchicalItem>.IndexOf(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			if (item is TreeMapItem value)
			{
				return collection.IndexOf(value);
			}
			return -1;
		}

		public TreeMapItemCollectionDecorator(TreeMapItemCollection collection)
		{
			this.collection = collection;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemLayout : ITreeMapItemLayout
	{
