		TreeMapRect ITreeMapItemLayout.Layout
		{
			get
			{
				return item.Layout.ToTreeMapRect();
			}
			set
			{
				item.Layout = value.ToRect();
			}
		}

		double ITreeMapItemLayout.Weight => item.Weight;

		public TreeMapItemLayout(ITreeMapLayoutItem item)
		{
			this.item = item;
		}
	}
	public static class TreeMapPanelLayoutHelper
	{
		public static Rect RoundChildLayout(Rect childLayout, Size finalSize)
		{
			double scaleX = ScreenHelper.ScaleX;
			if (scaleX == 1.0)
			{
				return RoundChildLayoutInternal(childLayout, finalSize);
			}
			Rect childLayout2 = new Rect(childLayout.X * scaleX, childLayout.Y * scaleX, childLayout.Width * scaleX, childLayout.Height * scaleX);
			Size finalSize2 = new Size(finalSize.Width * scaleX, finalSize.Height * scaleX);
			Rect rect = RoundChildLayoutInternal(childLayout2, finalSize2);
			return new Rect(rect.X / scaleX, rect.Y / scaleX, rect.Width / scaleX, rect.Height / scaleX);
		}

		private static Rect RoundChildLayoutInternal(Rect childLayout, Size finalSize)
		{
			double num = Math.Floor(childLayout.X);
			double num2 = Math.Floor(childLayout.Y);
			double value = childLayout.X + childLayout.Width - Math.Floor(childLayout.Width) - num;
			double value2 = childLayout.Y + childLayout.Height - Math.Floor(childLayout.Height) - num2;
			double num3 = ((Math.Round(value, 3) >= 1.0) ? (Math.Floor(childLayout.Width + 1.0) + 1.0) : (Math.Floor(childLayout.Width) + 1.0));
			double num4 = ((Math.Round(value2, 3) >= 1.0) ? (Math.Floor(childLayout.Height + 1.0) + 1.0) : (Math.Floor(childLayout.Height) + 1.0));
			num3 = ((num3 + num > finalSize.Width) ? (num3 - 1.0) : num3);
			num4 = ((num4 + num2 > finalSize.Height) ? (num4 - 1.0) : num4);
			return new Rect(num, num2, num3, num4);
		}
	}
	public static class TrigonometricUtils
	{
		public static double Radian2Degree(double angleRadian)
		{
			return angleRadian * 180.0 / Math.PI;
		}

		public static double Degree2Radian(double angleDegree)
		{
			return angleDegree * Math.PI / 180.0;
		}
	}
	public static class Extensions
	{
		public static Point MoveByAngle(this Point startPoint, double length, double angleRad)
		{
			return new Point(startPoint.X + Math.Cos(angleRad) * length, startPoint.Y + Math.Sin(angleRad) * length);
		}

		public static Color ToColor(this ColorARGB colorModel)
		{
			return Color.FromArgb(colorModel.A, colorModel.R, colorModel.G, colorModel.B);
		}

		public static ColorARGB ToColorARGB(this Color color)
		{
			return new ColorARGB(color.A, color.R, color.G, color.B);
		}

		public static Rect ToRect(this TreeMapRect treeMapRect)
		{
			return new Rect(treeMapRect.X, treeMapRect.Y, treeMapRect.Width, treeMapRect.Height);
		}

		public static TreeMapRect ToTreeMapRect(this Rect rect)
		{
			return new TreeMapRect(rect.X, rect.Y, rect.Width, rect.Height);
		}
	}
	public class CommonUtils
	{
		private const double brightnessThreshold = 0.7;

		public static bool IsColorBright(Color c)
		{
			return Math.Sqrt((double)(c.R * c.R) * 0.241 + (double)(c.G * c.G) * 0.691 + (double)(c.B * c.B) * 0.068) / 255.0 >= 0.7;
		}

		public static void SubscribePropertyChangedWeakEvent(INotifyPropertyChanged oldSource, INotifyPropertyChanged newSource, IWeakEventListener listener)
		{
			if (listener != null)
			{
				if (oldSource != null)
				{
					PropertyChangedWeakEventManager.RemoveListener(oldSource, listener);
				}
				if (newSource != null)
				{
					PropertyChangedWeakEventManager.AddListener(newSource, listener);
				}
			}
		}

		public static void SubscribeListChangedWeakEvent(IBindingList oldSource, IBindingList newSource, IWeakEventListener listener)
		{
			if (listener != null)
			{
				if (oldSource != null)
				{
					ListChangedEventManager.RemoveListener(oldSource, listener);
				}
				if (newSource != null)
				{
					ListChangedEventManager.AddListener(newSource, listener);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public static class ElementHelper
	{
		public static void ChangeOwner(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d != null && e.NewValue != e.OldValue)
			{
				if (e.OldValue is IOwnedElement ownedElement)
				{
					ownedElement.Owner = null;
				}
				if (e.NewValue is IOwnedElement ownedElement2)
				{
					ownedElement2.Owner = d;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class ViewControllerBase
	{
		private readonly ToolTipController toolTipController;

		protected IHitTestableElement HighlightedItemPresentation { get; private set; }

		protected HierarchicalChartControlBase HierarchicalControl { get; }

		public ViewControllerBase(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
		{
			HierarchicalControl = hierarchicalControl;
			toolTipController = new ToolTipController(hierarchicalControl);
		}

		private void UpdateSelection(IHitTestableElement item, ModifierKeys modifiers)
		{
			if (item != null)
			{
				if (item.Element is ISelectableItem item2 && HierarchicalControl.SelectionMode != SelectionMode.None)
				{
					HierarchicalControl.UpdateItemSelection(modifiers, item2);
				}
			}
			else if (modifiers == ModifierKeys.None)
			{
				ClearAllSelectedItems();
			}
		}

		private void ClearAllSelectedItems()
		{
			HierarchicalControl.ClearSelection();
		}

		private void UpdateHighLighting(IHitTestableElement currentItemPresentation)
		{
			if (HierarchicalControl.EnableHighlightingInternal && HighlightedItemPresentation != currentItemPresentation)
			{
				UnHighlightElements();
				SetHighLightedState(currentItemPresentation, isHighLighted: true);
			}
			HighlightedItemPresentation = currentItemPresentation;
		}

		protected abstract void UnHighlightElements();

		protected abstract void SetHighLightedState(IHitTestableElement hitTestableElement, bool isHighLighted);

		internal void UpdateSelection()
		{
			UpdateSelection(HighlightedItemPresentation, Keyboard.Modifiers);
		}

		internal void SetActualItemHighlighting(bool highlighted)
		{
			SetHighLightedState(HighlightedItemPresentation, highlighted);
		}

		internal void OnMouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			OnMouseMoveCore(e.GetPosition(HierarchicalControl));
		}

		internal void OnMouseMoveCore(Point hitPoint)
		{
			UpdateHighLighting(hitPoint);
			toolTipController.UpdateToolTip(hitPoint, ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseMove, HighlightedItemPresentation);
		}

		internal void OnMouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (HierarchicalControl.EnableHighlightingInternal)
			{
				SetActualItemHighlighting(highlighted: false);
			}
			HideToolTip();
			HighlightedItemPresentation = null;
		}

		internal void OnLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			OnLeftButtonUpCore(e.GetPosition(HierarchicalControl));
		}

		internal void OnLeftButtonUpCore(Point hitPoint)
		{
			UpdateSelection();
			toolTipController.UpdateToolTip(hitPoint, ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseUp, HighlightedItemPresentation);
		}

		internal void HideToolTip()
		{
			toolTipController.UpdateToolTip(default(Point), ToolTipNavigationAction.MouseLeave, null);
		}

		internal void UpdateHighLighting(Point position)
		{
			HierarchicalControlHitInfo hierarchicalControlHitInfo = HierarchicalControl.CalcHitInfoCore(position);
			UpdateHighLighting(hierarchicalControlHitInfo.Element);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapViewController : ViewControllerBase
	{
		public TreeMapViewController(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
			: base(hierarchicalControl)
		{
		}

		private void SetHighLighting(TreeMapItem item, bool isHighLighted)
		{
			if (item.Children.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (TreeMapItem child in item.Children)
				{
					SetHighLighting(child, isHighLighted);
				}
				return;
			}
			item.IsHighlighted = isHighLighted;
		}

		protected override void UnHighlightElements()
		{
			SetHighLightedState(base.HighlightedItemPresentation, isHighLighted: false);
		}

		protected override void SetHighLightedState(IHitTestableElement hitTestableElement, bool isHighLighted)
		{
			if (hitTestableElement != null)
			{
				SetHighLighting((TreeMapItem)hitTestableElement.Element, isHighLighted);
			}
		}
	}
	public class SunburstViewController : ViewControllerBase
	{
		private List<SunburstSectorViewInfo> highlightedElements = new List<SunburstSectorViewInfo>();

		private SunburstControl Sunburst => (SunburstControl)base.HierarchicalControl;

		public SunburstViewController(HierarchicalChartControlBase hierarchicalControl)
			: base(hierarchicalControl)
		{
		}

		private void SetElementHighlighted(SunburstSectorViewInfo sector)
		{
			sector.IsHighlighted = true;
			highlightedElements.Add(sector);
		}

		protected override void SetHighLightedState(IHitTestableElement hitTestableElement, bool isHighLighted)
		{
			if (!(hitTestableElement?.Element is SunburstSectorViewInfo sunburstSectorViewInfo))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!isHighLighted)
			{
				UnHighlightElements();
				return;
			}
			SetElementHighlighted(sunburstSectorViewInfo);
			SunburstHighlightMode highlightMode = Sunburst.HighlightMode;
			if (highlightMode != SunburstHighlightMode.PathFromRoot && highlightMode != SunburstHighlightMode.WholeGroup)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SunburstSectorViewInfo item in Sunburst.CollectRelativeItems(sunburstSectorViewInfo, highlightMode == SunburstHighlightMode.PathFromRoot))
			{
				SetElementHighlighted(item);
			}
		}

		protected override void UnHighlightElements()
		{
			for (int num = highlightedElements.Count - 1; num > -1; num--)
			{
				highlightedElements[num].IsHighlighted = false;
				highlightedElements.RemoveAt(num);
			}
		}
	}
	public class InteractionData
	{
		private readonly WeakReference treeMap;

		private TreeMapControl TreeMap => (TreeMapControl)treeMap.Target;

		public ITreeMapLayoutCalculator LayoutCalculator => TreeMap;

		public INativeItemsCollector Collector => TreeMap;

		public ITreeMapItemColorizer TreeMapItemColorizer => TreeMap;

		public InteractionData(TreeMapControl treeMap)
		{
			this.treeMap = new WeakReference(treeMap);
		}

		public void Invalidate()
		{
			TreeMap.InvalidateData();
		}
	}
}
